# Atelier — 原型

> **Status**: draft
> **Version**: v0.4
> **Created**: 2026-06-21 | **Updated**: 2026-07-05
> **落地目标**: Applet（终态跑在 Lynx 上，运行在 peers-touch Desktop 容器内）；**原型本身只是 React + LobeUI 的 web 展示**，不绑运行时
> **总账状态**: drafting（见 [原型总账](../../prototypes/README.md)）
> **Owner**: Peers-Touch Agent Team
> **Module**: `packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier/`

---

## 原型在哪

源码在 `packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier/`（统一原型工作区；独立 web 工程）。本目录只放入口说明，不复制源码（架构文档标准 §5.8）。

这个原型只为**展示 Atelier 终态产品长什么样**，用 React + LobeUI 写成网页给人确认形态；**不碰 Lynx / applet 容器 / SDK**。official applet product unit 已落在 `apps/applets/atelier/`，按 Lynx 运行时与 Station-bundled service binding 继续实现。

> **Atelier 本质是 applet，跑在 peers-touch Desktop 容器里**，不是独立全屏 app。容器外壳（全局 SideNav + 内容区 + applet pins）由 [desktop 容器外壳原型](../../desktop/prototype/README.md) 展示；本原型作为 workspace 包 `@peers-touch/prototype-desktop-atelier` 被 desktop 原型作为一个 applet 嵌入其内容区。本原型也可单独跑起来看 Atelier 自身界面（壳子高度自适应，`height: 100%`）。

P4-299 SDK Lynx bridge envelope robustness unit gate：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在为 Applet SDK Lynx bridge 提供 controlled/local unit matrix，覆盖 canonical object/string envelope unwrap、Host canonical error preservation、`bridge.invoke` 与 legacy `bridge.call` fallback、bridge-not-ready fail-closed、`events.subscribe` 无 params local long-poll、malformed event backoff，以及 SDK pending Host event replay bounded tail。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 写入 `official-applet/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.json`，并接入 completion readiness audit；该证据只证明 SDK bridge envelope robustness，不证明真实 Desktop Host Lynx injection、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-300 official status UI controlled unit gate：`tooling/scripts/atelier-official-status-ui-gate.mjs` 现在为 official applet 的 loading / empty / disconnected / auth-denied / error / reconciling / degraded / ready 状态矩阵输出独立 controlled/local evidence。该 gate 复用 `apps/applets/atelier/frontend/src/application/atelierViewState.test.ts`、`pageComposition.ts`、`officialRecoveryView.ts` 与 `AtelierAppletPage.tsx` 的 generated-contract coverage，证明 create-project 只属于 empty workspace、retry 只属于 retryable recovery、auth-denied 不 retry，且 retry boundary 只 reload Station projection，不暴露 provider/gate/artifact/memory/shell/file/input_snapshot。该证据不证明真实 Desktop product window UI、真实 Host failure producer、真实 Station projection stream failure matrix、post-ready applet UI actions 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-301 full E2E preflight controlled-gates status UI freshness guard：`tooling/acceptance/evidence/applets/official-applet/atelier-full-e2e-preflight.json` 现在记录的 `validatedScripts[atelier:controlled-gates].command` 已包含 `pnpm run atelier:official-status-ui-gate`。`tooling/scripts/atelier-projection-contract-gate.mjs` 新增静态锚点 `Full E2E preflight evidence must record official status UI gate in controlled gates script`，确保 controlled-gates 接入 status UI gate 后，preflight evidence 不能继续保留旧 command 快照；`tooling/scripts/atelier-completion-readiness-audit-controlled-gate.mjs` 同步新增 latest P4 docSync 负例。该证据只证明 controlled/local preflight command freshness，不提供真实 runtime inputs、不启动真实 Desktop、不连接真实 Station、不证明 post-ready applet UI actions 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-302 SDK Lynx requireModule bridge injection fallback guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 `NativeModules.bridge` 缺席时通过 `globalThis.lynx.requireModule('bridge')` 获取 Host bridge 的 fallback 路径，并验证 `isLynxEnvironment()` 识别该 runtime。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK Lynx bridge unit matrix covers lynx.requireModule bridge injection fallback when NativeModules is absent` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK bridge injection fallback unit behavior，不证明真实 Desktop Host Lynx bridge injection、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-303 SDK Lynx delayed bridge readiness tolerance guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 Lynx Web 先加载 applet bundle、Host bridge 后发布的时序：`waits for delayed Host bridge injection before readiness timeout` 用 fake timers 证明 `LynxBridgeAdapter.invoke()` 会在 readiness timeout 前继续轮询，bridge 注入后发送 canonical `{ method }` payload 并成功 unwrap。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK Lynx bridge unit matrix tolerates delayed Host bridge injection before readiness timeout` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK delayed bridge readiness tolerance，不证明真实 Desktop Host Lynx bridge injection timing、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-304 SDK legacy bridge.call string envelope unwrap guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 legacy `bridge.call('invoke', payload, callback)` callback 返回 JSON string envelope 的路径：`unwraps string envelopes returned by legacy bridge.call` 证明旧 Host callback 也会走 canonical envelope parse/unwrap，而不是只支持 object envelope。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK Lynx bridge unit matrix unwraps legacy bridge.call string envelopes before applet capability consumers observe results` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK legacy callback string envelope unwrap，不证明真实 Desktop Host legacy callback 注入、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-305 SDK legacy bridge.call string error envelope preservation guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 legacy `bridge.call('invoke', payload, callback)` callback 返回 JSON string error envelope 的路径：`preserves string error envelopes returned by legacy bridge.call` 证明旧 Host callback 的 canonical error 也会保留为 `AppletError`，包含 `code`、`message`、`details` 与 `requestId`，不会被吞成普通异常或成功值。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK Lynx bridge unit matrix preserves legacy bridge.call string error envelopes as AppletError before applet capability consumers observe failures` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK legacy callback string error envelope preservation，不证明真实 Desktop Host legacy callback 注入、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-306 SDK legacy bridge.call synchronous throw normalization guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 legacy `bridge.call('invoke', payload, callback)` 在调用时同步抛错的路径：`normalizes synchronous errors thrown by legacy bridge.call` 证明旧 Host callback transport 抛出的普通 `Error` 会被 `LynxBridgeAdapter.invoke()` 归一化为 `AppletErrorCode.InternalError` 的 `AppletError`，不会把未类型化异常直接暴露给 applet capability consumer。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK Lynx bridge unit matrix normalizes synchronous legacy bridge.call throws as AppletError before applet capability consumers observe failures` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK legacy callback synchronous throw normalization，不证明真实 Desktop Host legacy callback 注入、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-307 SDK events.subscribe canonical error retry guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 Host local long-poll `events.subscribe` 返回 canonical error envelope 的路径：`retries event long-poll after canonical Host error envelopes` 证明 SDK 不会把错误 envelope 当成投影事件派发，而是 backoff 后继续 long-poll，并在下一次正常事件到达时派发。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK Lynx bridge event receiver retries events.subscribe after canonical Host error envelopes without dispatching invalid events` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK event long-poll canonical error retry，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-308 SDK events.subscribe string canonical error retry guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 Host local long-poll `events.subscribe` 返回 JSON string canonical error envelope 的路径：`retries event long-poll after string canonical Host error envelopes` 证明 SDK 会先 parse string envelope，再按 canonical error backoff/retry，不会把字符串错误 envelope 当投影事件派发。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK Lynx bridge event receiver retries events.subscribe after string canonical Host error envelopes without dispatching invalid events` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK event long-poll string canonical error retry，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-309 SDK destroy bridge event teardown guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 `AppletSDK.destroy()` 的 bridge event teardown 路径：`tears down bridge event subscription and clears handlers on destroy` 证明 SDK 会调用 Host bridge unsubscribe、清空本地 handler/pending 状态，destroy 后旧 Host 事件不会继续进入已销毁 SDK 实例。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK destroy tears down bridge event subscription and clears local handlers before stale Host events can be retained` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK local teardown，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer、真实 product-window applet remount/unmount、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-310 SDK onEvent local handler unsubscribe guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖 `sdk.onEvent()` 返回的局部 unsubscribe 路径：`removes local event handlers when onEvent unsubscribe is called` 证明单个 topic handler 取消订阅后，同 topic 的后续 Host event 不会继续投递给该 handler。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK local event unsubscribe removes topic handlers before stale Host events can be delivered` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK local handler cleanup，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer、真实 product-window component unmount、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-311 SDK same-topic handler unsubscribe isolation guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 现在覆盖同 topic 多 handler 的局部 unsubscribe 隔离：`keeps sibling same-topic handlers after one onEvent unsubscribe` 证明取消第一个 `atelier.projection.event` handler 后，兄弟 same-topic handler 仍会收到后续 Host event。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK local event unsubscribe preserves sibling same-topic handlers for subsequent Host events` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK local handler identity cleanup 不误伤 sibling handler，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer、真实 product-window component unmount/remount、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-312 SDK dispatch self-unsubscribe isolation guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/index.ts` 的 local event dispatch 现在先 snapshot 同 topic handlers，再在每个 snapshot turn 前确认 handler 仍在注册表中；`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 新增 `keeps sibling same-topic handlers when one handler unsubscribes during dispatch`，证明第一个 `atelier.projection.event` handler 在处理事件时自取消，不会因为数组 `splice` 跳过兄弟 same-topic handler，且自取消 handler 不会收到下一次 Host event。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK local event dispatch snapshots same-topic handlers so self-unsubscribe cannot skip sibling handlers` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK local event dispatch mutation safety，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer、真实 product-window component unmount/remount、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-313 SDK dispatch new-handler deferral guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 新增 `defers same-topic handlers added during dispatch until the next Host event`，证明第一个 `atelier.projection.event` handler 在处理 `{ seq: 5 }` 时新增同 topic handler，该新增 handler 不会收到当前 in-flight Host event，只会从后续 `{ seq: 6 }` 开始接收；既有 sibling handler 仍正常收到两次事件。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK local event dispatch snapshots same-topic handlers so newly registered handlers wait for subsequent Host events` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK local event dispatch snapshot 对新增 handler 的当前事件隔离，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer、真实 product-window component mount timing、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

P4-314 SDK pending replay reentrant buffer guard：`packages/applet-sdk/src/index.ts` 的 `sdk.onEvent()` pending replay 现在先 snapshot 已排队 Host events，并清空当前 pending queue，再逐个 replay / requeue；`packages/applet-sdk/src/adapters/lynx.test.ts` 新增 `preserves pending Host events that arrive during pending replay`，证明 replay 已缓存 `atelier.projection.event` 时，如果 handler 触发新的 unmatched Host event，该新事件仍会留在 pending queue，随后注册对应 topic handler 可收到它。`tooling/scripts/atelier-sdk-lynx-bridge-unit-gate.mjs` 将 `Applet SDK pending Host event replay snapshots queued events so reentrant unmatched Host events remain buffered` 纳入 controlled/local evidence；completion readiness audit 与 controlled audit 负例同步消费该锚点。该证据只证明 SDK local pending replay reentrant buffering，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer reentrancy、真实 product-window mount timing、真实 Station SSE/Gateway delivery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E，readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

## 怎么跑

```bash
cd packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier
pnpm install        # 首次，monorepo 根装也可
pnpm dev            # Vite，浏览器打开 localhost
```

技术栈：React + Vite + `@lobehub/ui`(LobeUI) 优先 → antd 兜底 + `react-layout-kit` + `lucide-react` + `@peers-touch/applet-sdk` + CSS。当前入口已具备 **environment runtime bootstrap**：运行在 Lynx / Web Host applet 容器内时，UI 使用 applet-sdk bridge 调 `atelier.*` 真接口；普通浏览器 / Vite 独立预览时自动回退 mock runtime，不依赖 Station。Host 模式不应把 mock seed 当作真实首屏数据；真实 snapshot 返回前只能展示空壳 / loading / error 状态。

## 形态定调：对话优先（SOLO 式），不是团队项目管理

Atelier 是**个人** Agent 工作台，形态对标 TRAE Work / SOLO 的**对话优先**界面，刻意保持简单：左侧任务列表、中间一条对话流、底部一个大输入框；复杂任务时右侧才浮出 Todo 面板。

**不做**传统项目管理那套（项目树 + 看板 + DAG 计划图 + 多 Agent 协作侧栏）——那是给团队的，对个人太重。

Atelier 的灵魂（多 Agent 协商）不另开团队协作面板，而是**内联进对话流**：平时折叠成一行「🤝 N 个 Agent 协商了 X，已收敛」，点开看角色 / 带证据的反对 / 共识；只有需要你拍板时，决策卡才在对话流里冒出来。

## 任务管理是可插拔 plugin

「怎么组织我的任务」做成**可插拔 plugin**：同一份任务数据，换不同的 plugin 就换一种组织 / 展示方式（项目文件夹分组、简单平铺、Kanban、DAG……）。左栏顶部「管理方式」切换器选 plugin。

- **数据归壳子管，plugin 只渲染 + 委派**：壳子（Page）持有任务与生命周期；plugin 通过 `TaskHost`（`select / setStatus / purge / newTask`）回调改状态，不自己存数据。新增看板 / DAG 只需实现 `TaskPlugin.render`，对话流与数据模型不动。
- **默认 plugin = 项目文件夹分组（对齐 SOLO）**：任务按所属项目 / 仓库文件夹（`Task.project`）分组，每组可折叠、各带「+」新建，对齐 SOLO 的 "Your Task List"。简单平铺降为可选 plugin。
- **完整生命周期**：`active → archived → deleted`。进行中可归档 / 删除；归档可恢复 / 删除；删除进回收站可还原或彻底删除（`purge`）。归档段、回收站段作为分组下方可折叠段。
- 看板 / DAG 在切换器里作为「计划中」占位项，证明形态可换、未实现。

接口定义见 `src/types.ts` 的 `TaskHost` / `TaskPlugin`；plugin 实现见 `src/plugins.tsx`。

## Single-column 外壳对齐（Browser prototype synced with official product shape）

以 SOLO 的对话优先人体工学为基准线，在其上叠 Atelier 独有的协商 / 决策 / 预算灵魂。当前 browser prototype 已和 official Lynx applet 的 controlled single-column projection shell 对齐：Task organizer、provider capability、projection stream、Project Health / TaskGraph / Context、Artifacts preview 都在同一 content rail 内纵向堆叠；不再把 left rail / right preview panel 作为默认产品形态。

- **Header / action rail**：任务标题、project/status metadata、预算条、New task、Open in IDE intent、Work / Code / Design intent preset 与 Host-intent-only disclosure 均在 content rail 顶部；Open in IDE 仍只是 Host `workspace.open` intent，不暴露 file/shell/execute。
- **Task organizer section**：原 left rail 的 "Your Task List"、plugin 切换器、项目文件夹分组与 lifecycle sections 收进单列 section；plugin 只渲染和委派 `TaskHost` intent。
- **Provider capability section**：Skills / provider capabilities 是只读 Station capability projection；点击只插入 slash command，执行仍归 Station / Agent orchestration。
- **Projection stream**：轻 markdown（`` `code` `` / **粗体** / 列表）、完成回复带 Completed + 反馈条（赞/踩/复制/重新生成）、"N files changed +X -Y" diff 卡（可展开文件列表）、图片附件 chip、Artifact 卡。
- **Workspace projection section**：Project Health、TaskGraph、Context、legacy Todo fallback 在同一 content rail 内纵向展示。Applet 只消费 `workspace.projects[].taskGraph` / legacy todo projection，不调度、执行或重排节点。
- **Artifacts 托盘 + 内联预览 section**：composer 上方一排 Artifacts 卡片（markdown / web / image / diff，按 kind 配紫色图标）；点卡片在单列 content rail 中打开**产物预览 section**。当前 browser prototype 与 official projection boundary 对齐为 metadata-only：不从 projection 读取/渲染 markdown、iframe、image、diff、URL 或 source raw body/ref；只展示 Host safe text fetch、Host sandbox preview intent、projected paths/size 与 mock Console Logs disclosure。
- **富输入框**：斜杠命令 / 图片附件 / run target selector / 发送。语音输入暂不展示。

## Runtime / Projection 接入骨架

原型已从“Page 直接改 mock state”收敛到一个可联调边界：

- `src/runtime.ts` 定义 `AtelierRuntime`，这是 UI 和未来真实后端之间的唯一边界。
- `src/projection.ts` 消费当前 `atelier-projection/v0` product-unit contract 的 generated constants，定义 browser prototype adapter 的 projection snapshot / patch / method 类型视图；它不是一套独立草案协议。
- `src/bridgeRuntime.ts` 定义 bridge-backed runtime，后续可以接 Station、Tauri command 或 applet-sdk。
- `src/appletBridge.ts` 定义 applet-sdk / Web Host 适配器，把 `sdk.invoke('atelier.*')` 和 host event 转成 `AtelierRuntimeBridge`。
- `src/runtimeBootstrap.ts` 定义入口 runtime 选择：`sdk.runtime === 'lynx' | 'web-host'` 时创建 bridge runtime；`standalone / unavailable` 时回退 `createMockAtelierRuntime()`。
- `createMockAtelierRuntime()` 仍使用 `src/mock.ts` seed 数据，但所有状态变更都通过 runtime 方法发生。
- `src/Page.tsx` 只消费 `AtelierRuntimeSnapshot`，新增任务、发送消息、决策选择、归档 / 删除、模型切换都调用 runtime；如果 runtime 提供 `subscribe`，Page 会订阅 projection 更新。Page 已显式区分 `loading / empty / reconciling / degraded / disconnected / auth-denied / error / ready`，断连、权限拒绝、投影同步中和降级状态不会伪装成“无任务”。
- 真实联调时，`src/main.tsx` 会通过 `createAtelierRuntimeForEnvironment()` 自动装配 `createBridgeAtelierRuntime()` + `createAppletSdkAtelierBridge(sdk)`，对接已登记的 `atelier.*` runtime methods。
- UI 期望拿到的是 projection snapshot：`TaskList`、`ProjectProjection[]`、`StreamBlock[]`、`TodoProjection`、`ContextProjection`、`ArtifactProjection`、`GateProjection`；不要让 applet 直接解析裸 orchestration event。`workspace.projects` 是 Station 投影出的只读 Project/MilestoneTree/TaskGraph/Policy/Defect 视图，供后续插件 / 详情面板消费；默认对话优先界面不因该字段回退成项目管理看板。
- Station 已有第一批真源接口：`POST /sub-agent/agent/atelier/workspace/load` 和 `POST /sub-agent/agent/atelier/project/create-from-goal`。
- Desktop applet gateway 已开放最小 `atelier` capability / service surface：official workspace load 走 Station-bundled `atelier` service binding（`sdk.network.request({ service: 'atelier', path: '/v1/workspace' })`），project create / message / escalation / task lifecycle 也走 `/v1/...` service path；provider discovery、feedback、artifact body fetch 走 service binding；workspace open 与 artifact preview 保持 Host-local capability；projection event subscribe 保持 Host event broker + Station stream。
- Projection 增量事件 topic 为 `atelier.projection.event`，Desktop gateway 会把 Station `/agent/events/subscribe` SSE 转成 `AtelierProjectionEvent` 并放入 applet event outbox。

### Projection 契约纪律

当前 projection 是 `atelier-projection/v0` product-unit contract 对齐下的联调契约，不是跨版本生产稳定协议。为避免 TS / Rust / Go 三边结构漂移，后续每次改 projection 字段或 patch kind 必须同步检查：

- TypeScript：`packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier/src/projection.ts`
- Rust Desktop gateway：`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/application/applets/mod.rs` 中 `station_event_to_atelier_projection_event`
- Go Station projection：`apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service/atelier_projection.go`
- Manifest / capability：`apps/applets/atelier/applet.manifest.json` 与 `packages/applet-contract/src/capability.ts`
- Docs：本文的 Runtime method 与联调约定

`apps/applets/atelier/contracts/atelier-projection.contract.json` 是当前 `atelier-projection/v0` 的 product-unit machine-readable contract，覆盖 projection version、event topic、Host capability methods、Desktop gateway actions、patch union 与 replay metadata。P2-06 已从该文件生成 `atelier-projection.schema.generated.json`、official frontend `projection.contract.generated.ts` 与 browser prototype `projection.contract.generated.ts`，并新增正式 projection proto `model/domain/atelier/v1/projection.proto` 及 Station Go / Desktop TS 生成物；JSON contract 继续负责 Host/Gateway 方法与 forbidden actions，proto 负责 snapshot/event/patch 类型锚点。official reducer 与 browser bridge runtime 的 patch apply switch 现在带 `assertNever(patch)`，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 会 exact 比对 contract `patchKinds` 与两侧 non-snapshot handler cases，避免新增 patch kind 只出现在 parser/type 中却没有 projection consumer 行为。当前仍不能把 `atelier-projection/v0` 宣称为跨版本稳定协议；只能宣称“当前三端实现由 product-unit contract + formal proto + generated schema/TS constants + gate 对齐”。当前 gate：

```bash
pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate
pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate
pnpm run atelier:desktop-bridge-gate
pnpm run atelier:desktop-typecheck-gate
pnpm run atelier:prototype-gate
pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate
pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-build-gate
pnpm run applet:atelier-desktop-injection-gate
pnpm run atelier:controlled-gates
pnpm run atelier:real-product-gates
```

`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 读取 product-unit contract，先执行 `atelier:projection-codegen-check`，再检查 formal projection proto、Station Go / Desktop TS proto 生成物、prototype TS、official frontend guard、Go Station projection、Rust Desktop gateway、manifest 与 shared capability registry 没有明显漂移。`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 执行真实 TS runtime 逻辑，覆盖 snapshot/event guard、重复 event 去重、同 scope 过期 seq 拒绝、snapshot 与非 snapshot capability 的异步错误状态映射（`auth-denied` / `disconnected` / `error`），以及非 snapshot capability 成功后恢复调用前 runtime status。
`atelier:desktop-bridge-gate` 复用 Desktop applet bridge/lifecycle 测试（`apps/desktop/src/applet/*.test.ts`），覆盖 `<lynx-host>` Host command side-effect 剥离、manager-owned Gateway session lifecycle、Kernel-owned pause/resume 与 applet runtime adapter 行为；该 gate 仍是本地 controlled runtime test，不证明真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
`atelier:desktop-typecheck-gate` 运行 Desktop app TypeScript check（`pnpm --dir apps/desktop run prebuild:check`），覆盖 Desktop applet Host/page wiring 的类型一致性；它不证明真实 Desktop runtime 行为。
`atelier:prototype-gate` 构建 browser prototype（`packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier`），作为 prototype sync 的可重复本地证据；它不证明 official Lynx runtime 或真实 Host 渲染。
`atelier:official-frontend-build-gate` 构建 official Lynx frontend bundle（`apps/applets/atelier/frontend/dist/main.lynx.bundle`），证明 official applet frontend 当前可打包；它不证明 Desktop 注入、Host bridge 或 Station service runtime。
`applet:atelier-desktop-injection-gate` 执行 official applet static packaging / Desktop injection / manifest integrity / service binding 校验，证明构建产物可被 Desktop dist registry 静态识别；它不启动真实 Desktop Host、Station service 或 product window。
`atelier:controlled-gates` 聚合上述 controlled gates，并追加 Desktop TypeScript check、browser prototype build、official frontend `tsc --noEmit`、official Lynx bundle build 与 Desktop injection static gate，作为进入真实 E2E 前的本地非 E2E evidence suite；它仍不证明真实 Station/Host/product window 链路。
`atelier:real-product-gates` 当前聚合 Atelier real-product workspace/replay gate、normal product-window gate、cross-restart cursor gate、human-decision/live-resume gate、artifact/gate product-window gate、product-window safe artifact body fetch gate 与 blocking-gate recovery variant gates：workspace/replay gate 启动 sqlite-backed Station gate server，经 Desktop Gateway `network.request` / `atelier.events.subscribe` 访问 Station-bundled Atelier façade 与 Station EventStreamService；normal product-window gate 启动 packaged `peers.atelier` Desktop product shell，通过 external Station proxy 断言 official applet 在真实 Desktop product window UI 内完成 `GET /v1/workspace` service binding 到 `/applets/atelier/v1/workspace`，再通过 product-window E2E launch options 触发 `atelier.events.subscribe`，等待 `/sub-agent/agent/events/subscribe`，并用 gate server replay probe 证明受控 pre-replay / post-first-replay SSE close 后的 cursor replay 序列 `afterEventSeq=0 -> []`、`afterEventSeq=0 -> [1]`、`afterEventSeq=1 -> [2]` 到达 Station EventStreamService，同时覆盖 applet-side rendered state assertion after projection event delivery；cross-restart cursor recovery 由独立 product-window cross-restart gate 使用同一 storage root、不同 applet session 证明持久化 cursor 恢复；artifact/gate gates 覆盖 Station-produced artifact/gate projection、Host-rendered preview surface descriptor、metadata-only safe body fetch、`rerun_failed_node` Station orchestration provider recovery completion，以及 `accept_risk` / `continue` / `cancel` recovery resolve variants。它仍不证明任意网络故障矩阵、Host rich artifact renderer / Console Logs / attachment Host Storage 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Real-product evidence claim boundary 当前有 static/evidence-schema guard：`applet-atelier-real-product-gate.mjs`、`applet-atelier-product-window-gate.mjs`、decision / live-resume / cross-restart / artifact-gate focused product-window gates 写出的 evidence 都显式包含 `claimBoundary.readiness=NOT_READY`、`claimBoundary.proves` 与 `claimBoundary.doesNotProve`，且 `notCovered` 复用 `doesNotProve`，避免把 workspace/replay、product-window 或 focused recovery 局部 REAL_PRODUCT_PATH 证据升级为全局 Atelier ready。既有 `official-applet/atelier-*.json` evidence 也已按现有 `coveredPaths/notCovered` 机械回填 claimBoundary，并由 `atelier-projection-contract-gate` 动态扫描 JSON 保证 `proves === coveredPaths`、`doesNotProve === notCovered`。`applet-atelier-desktop-injection-gate.mjs` 也写出 static-only claim boundary，只证明 manifest / service binding / Station route / bundle integrity 注入事实；source manifest 的 `sha256:pending-atelier-bundle` 被显式标记为 expected placeholder，dist manifest 才是 generated bundle integrity truth，不证明 product-window runtime、Station projection stream 或完整 E2E。该改动不改变 prototype UI、official applet runtime 行为或真实 gate 覆盖面。

GAP-01~07 的 Project 族 projection 已进入 Station JSON mapper 与 official/prototype guard：`workspace.projects` 包含 `ProjectProjection`，并从 Station-owned `CollaborationTask`、`TaskNode` 与 durable events / query indexes 派生 `MilestoneTree`、`TaskGraph`、`openBlockers`、`residualRisks`、`memoryCandidates`、`Policy` 与 `Defect` read-only projections。Project/Milestone `state` 由 Station-owned `ProjectStateMachine` 在 durable outbox transaction 内从 task/node/gate/blocker/risk/predicate/memory evidence 推进并写入 `agent_project_states`；同一 runtime 还会 materialize `agent_atelier_milestones`、`agent_atelier_task_graph_nodes`、`agent_atelier_task_graph_edges`、`agent_atelier_policies`、`agent_atelier_policy_rules` 与 `agent_atelier_defects`，`LoadWorkspace` 优先使用这些 Station-owned persisted MilestoneTree/TaskGraph/Policy/Defect records，旧 node/event/meta-derived projection 仅作为历史数据 fallback。prototype/official applet 只消费 projection 字段，不在 UI 内重新判定状态或生产 Policy/Defect。`ProjectCompletion.noOpenBlockers` 已从旧的 blocker 数量判断推进到 `Blocker.state` 谓词：blocking gate failed 产生 `open` blocker，gate-blocked human decision / later passed gate result 可把同一 gate blocker 推进为 `resolved`，`accept_risk` 映射为 `waived`；failed gate 会 materialize proposed Defect query record，供 projection 只读展示，不代表完整 Defect governance lifecycle。L0/L1 gate acceptance 按 gate id 取 latest durable gate result，避免历史失败 gate 永久污染已重跑通过的项目。Station 侧已新增 `agent_project_states` / `agent_project_blockers` / `agent_project_residual_risks` / `agent_acceptance_predicates` query index：TaskEventWriter 从 durable outbox 同步 Project/Milestone legacy state payload、blocker/risk lifecycle 与 acceptance predicate registry/verdict snapshot，随后刷新 deterministic predicates 并推进 ProjectStateMachine；`LoadWorkspace` 优先使用这些 Station-owned persisted records，旧 meta/event-derived projection 仅作为历史数据 fallback。Station deterministic `AcceptancePredicateEvaluator` 可计算 no_open_blockers、data-model blocker quantifier、root milestone/task accepted、residual risks、goal owner signoff、memory candidates、`gate.passed`、`gate_result(g).passed`、blocking gate aggregate 与 L2 human signoff aggregate；root milestone accepted 不读取旧 ProjectState 自证，避免 stale-cycle。Milestone 只投影 `acceptancePredicateIds`，ProjectCompletion 只投影聚合布尔值，Applet 不获得 predicate registry、evaluator 或 ProjectStateMachine 执行权。TaskGraph node 的 `artifactIds/gateIds` 由 Station-owned `TaskArtifact` / `TaskGateResult` indexes 按 `step_id` 回链；缺少 node 归属的 artifact/gate 仍保持 task-level projection，避免 UI 猜测 evidence 归属。这些字段仍然只是 projection surface；Applet 不获得 Project persistence、TaskGraph production、Policy engine、Defect lifecycle 或 Agent orchestration 的执行权。

`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 执行 official Lynx applet 的 projection guard / reducer 逻辑，覆盖 `atelier.projection.event` 解析、重复 event 去重、同 scope 过期 seq 拒绝、`stream.append` block 去重，以及 `artifact.upsert` / `gate.upsert` patch 应用；它只证明前端 projection consumer 的本地行为，不证明真实 Host + Station SSE 已跑通。

### Runtime method 草案

| Method | 用途 |
|--------|------|
| `GET /v1/workspace` service binding | 加载当前 workspace projection snapshot |
| `atelier.project.createFromGoal` | 从用户目标创建 Atelier project / task |
| `atelier.message.send` | 向当前 task 追加用户输入 |
| `atelier.escalation.resolve` | 回写决策卡选择 |
| `atelier.task.setStatus` | 更新左栏收纳态：active / archived / deleted |
| `atelier.task.purge` | 彻底删除回收站任务 |

`AtelierRuntime.setModel()` 只更新 composer 本地偏好，不走 Host / Station capability。

### applet-sdk 接入草案

真实 applet 侧不直接 import Desktop `api` 或 Tauri `invoke`，而是把 applet-sdk 的 `sdk` 适配成 `AtelierRuntimeBridge`。当前 `src/main.tsx` 已使用 `src/runtimeBootstrap.ts` 做环境切换，等价于：

```ts
import { sdk } from '@peers-touch/applet-sdk';
import { MOCK } from './mock';
import { createAppletSdkAtelierBridge } from './appletBridge';
import { createBridgeAtelierRuntime } from './bridgeRuntime';
import { toProjectionSnapshot } from './projection';

export const runtime = createBridgeAtelierRuntime({
  bridge: createAppletSdkAtelierBridge(sdk),
  initialSnapshot: toProjectionSnapshot(MOCK),
});
```

联调约定：

- 命令：`sdk.invoke(method, payload)`，其中 `method` 使用上表的 `atelier.*` 点分方法名。
- 启动：Host adapter 为 `lynx` / `web-host` 时走 bridge；`standalone` / `unavailable` 时走 mock，保证 prototype 可以继续独立设计评审。Host 模式的初始 snapshot 必须是空 projection，不允许展示 mock 任务后再被真实数据覆盖。
- 权限：`@peers-touch/applet-contract` 已登记 `atelier.*` capability methods；运行时 manifest 草案位于 `apps/applets/atelier/applet.manifest.json`。
- 订阅：如需自动连接 Station SSE，可通过 URL 参数 `agentId`、可选 `taskId` / `afterEventSeq`，或在页面注入 `window.__ATELIER_PROJECTION_STREAM__`，由 `runtimeBootstrap` 传给 `createAppletSdkAtelierBridge()`。
- 返回：每个命令先返回完整 `AtelierProjectionSnapshot`，保证 UI 能从任意一次操作恢复一致状态。
- 增量：host 通过 `atelier.projection.event` 推送 `AtelierProjectionEvent`，其中 `patch` 使用 `AtelierProjectionPatch`；bridge runtime 会按 event `id` / `seq` 幂等消费，并在 `stream.append` 时按 block `id` 去重，抵御 SSE 重连回放 / outbox 重投。
- 健壮性：`src/projection.ts` 提供 `assertAtelierProjectionSnapshot()` 与 `parseAtelierProjectionEvent()`；snapshot guard 会校验 `tasks` task envelope、`streams/todos/contexts/artifacts/gates` record keys 与 value envelope，避免 malformed task snapshot 或 snapshot bucket 绕过 event patch guard；`task.upsert` event 也复用同一 task envelope guard，拒绝空 id、非法 lifecycle status 与非 `pt-workspace://` open target；`artifact.upsert` / `gate.upsert` event 复用 artifact/gate envelope guard，拒绝 artifact body leak、非法 previewTarget、空 gate check 与非法 artifactIds。official frontend `domain/projection.ts` 同步拒绝 malformed task snapshot、malformed snapshot bucket、malformed replay cursor、空 task-scoped event、非法 task upsert 与 malformed stream/todo/context/artifact/gate patch，保持 browser prototype 与 Lynx official projection ingress parity。`src/appletBridge.ts` 会 guard command 返回的 snapshot，但 projection event payload 原样转交 runtime；`src/bridgeRuntime.ts` 统一 parse Host event、拒绝 malformed event 与同 scope 过期 seq，并把 snapshot 与非 snapshot capability 的异步 bridge 错误映射为 `auth-denied / disconnected / error` runtime status；非 snapshot capability 成功后会恢复调用前 status，避免 provider discovery / artifact fetch 等成功路径残留 `loading`。
- 收纳生命周期：`apps/applets/atelier/contracts/atelier-projection.contract.json` 已新增 `taskLifecycle` 机器契约，声明 `status` 属于 `workbench_lifecycle` 且 `orthogonalTo=execution_state`，states 为 `active/archived/deleted`，所有 transitions reversible，`atelier.task.purge` 只允许 `deleted`；codegen 导出 `ATELIER_TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATES` / `AtelierTaskLifecycleStatus`，prototype projection guard、official UI normalize 与 official `setAtelierTaskStatus` client input 均从该契约读取，不维护本地 lifecycle union。
- Projection taxonomy：`apps/applets/atelier/contracts/atelier-projection.contract.json` 现在也承载 `projectSurface` 与 `workbenchSurface` 机器契约；Project / Milestone / TaskGraph / Blocker / Risk / Memory / Policy / Defect guard，以及 task intent / todo / context group / artifact kind / gate status 等 workbench projection enum，都经 codegen 生成 official / browser prototype TS constants 后由两侧 projection guard 消费。prototype 不再维护这些本地 allowlist，official 也不再用 string-only 宽松校验替代 contract-backed enum；这仍只证明 projection ingress taxonomy parity，不证明 Station producer runtime 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。
- 真源：Station / agent orchestration 负责把底层 collaboration task、run、event、artifact、gate、decision 转成 projection；Atelier applet 只消费 projection。
- Artifact / Gate / Provider：`block_kind=artifact` 会投影为 `artifact.upsert`，`block_kind=gate_result` 会投影为 `gate.upsert`。`SubmitCollaborationNodeResultRequest.artifacts / gates` 已升级为 proto/DTO 强类型入口，legacy `meta.artifacts_json / meta.gates_json` 仅作 fallback；Gate result 已有独立 `TASK_EVENT_TYPE_GATE_RESULT` 真源。Artifact evidence policy 已在 Station 入站和 outbox index 两层收敛，`agent_task_artifacts` 会保存 event_seq / uri / checksum / refs_json / sanitized payload_json 等证据索引；artifact body 已从 outbox/projection 分离到 Station-owned `agent_task_artifact_blobs`，`TaskEventWriter.appendTx` 会在写 `agent_task_events` 前抽取 `markdown/content/body/html/diff/patch`，outbox 只保留 `body_ref / body_hash / body_size / body_kind / body_retention_* / preview_hint` metadata；`atelier.artifact.body.fetch` 已作为显式 service binding 接入 official applet 与 Station route，只返回 `markdown/diff/text/json` safe text，Station 按 owner、canonical body ref、`task_id + artifact_id + body_uri`、retention/expiry/hash/max-bytes 校验读取 blob。Station-owned `GateRunner` / `GateCheckExecutor`、typed `TaskGatePlan` proto、`agent_task_gate_plans` persistence 和 active GatePlan 静态调度路径均已接入；Provider/Gate typed proto/DTO 已继续收敛，`TaskProviderPlan` / `TaskProviderSpec.agent_id/role` 已接入 `CreateCollaborationTask` 和 Atelier `project.createFromGoal`，并持久化到 `agent_task_provider_plans`；Station execution 已消费 persisted provider plan 覆盖 `TurnConfig.Provider/Model/Effort` 与 conversation metadata；`agent_direct_runs` 已作为 DirectRun first-class persistence record 接入同事务创建和 purge；`TASK_SURFACE_DIRECT_RUN`、no-session `TaskRun`、`ExecutionStep(PENDING/STATION_HOSTED)` 与 durable `TASK_CREATED` marker event 已接入 service-level；runtime preflight 会 fail-closed 校验 provider/model/budget/policy/trace refs 与 pending step，recovery 会跳过 DirectRun 的普通 collaboration 恢复，但 Provider execution 尚未启动；`TaskGateType` / `TaskGateCheckType` / `TaskGateEvaluatorSpec` / `TaskProviderSpec` 会被 `GateRunner` 优先消费，legacy `meta.agent_ids`、`check_type/gate_type/evaluator/level/config_json` 只作为 fallback。blocking gate 失败会暂停 task 并保留 `gate_blocked*` meta，服务级恢复/复跑见下一条。`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 已守住 artifact/gate/provider production method 不暴露给 applet、Station node-result typed request、CreateTask typed provider plan、TaskEventWriter durable outbox、Artifact policy/evidence/blob index、projection body redaction、official safe text artifact body fetch、GateRunner、GatePlan typed fields、active GatePlan 调度、provider runtime override、DirectRun persistence record、DirectRun no-session marker、runtime preflight/recovery split 和 blocking gate recovery contract。剩余是真实 rich artifact preview capability、真实 DirectRun Provider execution 与 E2E 验证。
- Public typed node-result fixture：公开 typed `SubmitCollaborationNodeResult` service fixture 已覆盖 desktop executor agent + active lease + typed artifact/gate 提交，验证 durable outbox 顺序、artifact evidence index、artifact body blob redaction、gate result index、blocking gate 暂停与 lease release；typed artifact refs policy 已覆盖 self-ref、artifact URI ref 与 empty ref 的事务前拒绝，空 ref 不再在 DTO 转换层被静默丢弃。`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 已把 public typed node-result fixture 与 invalid refs rollback 纳入机器检查。
- Gate result index：`agent_task_gate_results` 已作为 durable query index 接入 `TaskEventWriter.appendTx`，与 `agent_task_events` 同事务同步；覆盖正式 `TASK_EVENT_TYPE_GATE_RESULT` 和 legacy `TURN_EVENT + block_kind=gate_result`，索引 task/step/turn/event/event_seq/gate/gate_plan/status/blocking/artifact_ids/checks/produced_by/payload_json，用于 replay/debug 查询但不替代 durable outbox 真源。`atelier.task.purge` 会同步清理 gate result index、artifact index、gate plans 和 interrupt lifecycle records，避免删除 task 后留下孤儿证据行。
- Realtime envelope lineage：`TaskEventWriter.Publish`、interrupt request/resolve 手动 publish、committed task event publish 与 replay `taskEventRecordToDomainEvent` 都会在 EventBus metadata 中带出 durable `event_id` / `event_seq` / `task_id`，projection replay 测试固定 metadata `event_id` 与 `DomainEvent.EventID` 一致；`TaskEvent.collaboration_session_event` 已作为 typed proto session lifecycle projection 接入，只从 durable explicit `collaboration_session_event` payload 投影，不从 `result_summary` fallback 推断。这只强化实时 fanout / replay 的 lineage envelope 与 typed session event schema，不改变 `agent_task_events` durable outbox 真源，也不等同真实 schedule 流转、真实 SSE reconnect/cross-restart E2E 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
- Blocking gate recovery：Station service-level 已通过既有 `atelier.escalation.resolve` / interrupt resolution 路径接入恢复/复跑，不新增 `atelier.gate.*` applet method。恢复动作支持 `continue / rerun / accept / cancel`：清理 `gate_blocked*` meta，按动作更新 blocked GatePlan 为 `continued / active / accepted / cancelled`，rerun 会把 blocked node 重置为 pending，cancel 会取消 task 并 skip pending nodes；恢复前拒绝未过期 active executor lease，过期 lease 会标为 expired 后继续恢复。`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 已覆盖 forbidden applet recovery method、recovery helper/action strings、guarded resume、active/expired lease fence 与服务测试存在性。真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E gate recovery 仍未验证。
- 当前已落地：official frontend 的 workspace / project create / message / escalation / task lifecycle 走 Station-bundled `atelier` service binding（`GET /v1/workspace`、`POST /v1/projects`、`POST /v1/messages`、`POST /v1/escalations:resolve`、task status/purge `/v1/tasks/...`）；provider discovery、feedback、memory confirmation、rerun confirmation 与 artifact body fetch 已收敛到 `/v1` service binding；workspace open、artifact preview open 仍是 Host-local intent；events subscribe 仍是 Host event broker + Station stream。创建项目要求 payload 显式传 `agentIds` 或 `run.agentIds`；official Lynx UI 只从 Host launch/global 配置读取显式 `agentIds` / `agentId`，缺失时展示 `agentIds` required 错误，Atelier 不从 `flowId` 或模型选择猜 Agent。
- 模型选择：`AtelierRuntime.setModel()` 在 mock / bridge runtime 内本地更新 snapshot，不调用 Desktop gateway；official Lynx UI 已补新任务模型意图选择器，只把选择写入 `atelier.project.createFromGoal` 的 `run.model`，不创建「直接模型」旁路，也不持久化模型偏好。后续如需跨设备保存偏好，应单独设计 settings capability，而不是伪造 orchestration 接口。
- `atelier.message.send` 会写入 Station `agent_task_events`，并标记为 `block_kind=user`；snapshot replay 和 `atelier.projection.event` 都会还原为用户消息块。
- `atelier.escalation.resolve` 会委托 Station orchestration 在同一事务内完成 guarded resume 与 `agent_task_events` outbox append；事件类型为 `TASK_EVENT_TYPE_INTERRUPT_RESOLVED`，payload 标记 `block_kind=decision_resolved` 并携带 `resume_payload_json`；snapshot replay 和 `atelier.projection.event` 都会还原为 `decision.resolved` patch。`agent_interrupt_requests` 已作为 durable lifecycle store 接入 outbox writer 和 AutoMigrate / migration：`RequestCollaborationInterrupt` 写 `INTERRUPT_REQUESTED` + pending，requested event 会在 snapshot replay / `atelier.projection.event` 中投影为 `decision` block；`ResolveCollaborationInterrupt` 写 `INTERRUPT_RESOLVED` + resolved / `resume_payload_json` / `resolved_at`，resolved event 会按 `interrupt_id` / `block_id` 回填 chosen。恢复后第一个 Station-hosted turn 会读取未消费的 resolved interrupt，把 `resume_payload_json` 作为权威人工决策上下文注入 prompt，并在 turn 成功后把 `consumed_at / consumed_step_id / consumed_turn_id` 写回 `agent_interrupt_requests`；失败重试不会丢失该 payload。事务提交后才发布 realtime bus event，并且只有真实恢复时才重新进入 orchestration loop。当前受控 resume 规则：`RUNNING` task 为 no-op 但可记录 resolved event，`PAUSED` 且没有 running node 的 task 会恢复为 `RUNNING`，`PAUSED` 但仍有 running node 时只有 Station-owned live resume broker 已注册同一 `task_id + interrupt_id` waiter 才会写 durable resolved event 并在提交后唤醒等待 turn，且不启动新的 execution loop；没有 matching waiter 时仍在同一事务内拒绝且不写 resolved event，以避免重复执行和错误投影。
- `atelier.task.setStatus` 会更新 `CollaborationTask.MetaJSON.atelier_status`，支持 `active / archived / deleted`；`atelier.task.purge` 仅允许删除状态任务，执行后清理 task、nodes、events。official Lynx UI 对 purge 额外要求二次确认点击，但删除前置条件仍由 Station 强制校验。
- 当前已落地：`atelier.events.subscribe`，要求 payload 显式传 `agentId`，可选 `taskId` / `afterEventSeq`；订阅后通过 `events.poll` 读取 `atelier.projection.event`。Desktop gateway 已有 session-level subscription registry：同 session / agent / task 重复订阅会复用既有 reader，`events.unsubscribe(topic=atelier.projection.event)` 和 session cleanup 会标记取消；reader 会按 registry 退出，阻塞中的 Station SSE 请求会在有界 timeout 后关闭连接，断线后按 bounded backoff 重连，并使用 Host runtime 持久化的 `last_event_seq` 作为下一次 cursor。official Lynx applet 已通过 `sdk.events.on/subscribe('atelier.projection.event')` 消费 Host outbox 事件，并且会等 `/v1/workspace` service binding 返回 snapshot 后再建立 Station stream：如果启动参数或 `globalThis.__ATELIER_PROJECTION_STREAM__` 提供显式 `agentId`，会直接调用 `atelier.events.subscribe`；如果只提供显式 `agentIds` 数组，则使用 `agentIds[0]` 作为默认 stream `agentId`，仍不从 `flowId`、模型选择或历史任务推断 Agent。若 Host 未显式提供 `taskId / afterEventSeq`，official Lynx applet 会使用当前 selected task 及 `workspace.replay[taskId].nextEventSeq` 作为默认 replay cursor，避免重复拉取 snapshot 已折叠的事件；订阅 effect 只按 selected task / replay cursor 变化重建，不会因普通 projection patch 更新 snapshot 对象而自激重连。`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 已固定 `subscriptionMethod` 不得进入 `runtimeMethods`、`intentKind=projection_event_subscription`、official cursor 必须来自 `workspace.replay[taskId].nextEventSeq`，且显式或派生的非正数 cursor（包含 `0` 和负数）不写入 subscribe payload。
- 当前不足：Station 端取消依赖 SSE 连接关闭触发 `ctx.Done()` / unsubscribe，Desktop 已实现有界连接关闭但尚未做真实 Host + Station + applet 端到端取消验证；Desktop cursor 已持久化，official/prototype frontend 已能从 snapshot replay cursor 建立订阅，real-product gate 已覆盖 `afterEventSeq` 经 Desktop Gateway 到 Station `EventStreamService` 的 cursor-filtered replay，但尚未做真实跨重启 Host + Station + applet 端到端恢复验证。
- P2-03 official projection stream config normalization 也有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 现在对 launch options / `globalThis.__ATELIER_PROJECTION_STREAM__` 中的 explicit `agentId`、generated `agentIds[0]` fallback 和 explicit `taskId` 做 trim，空白 explicit `agentId` 不会阻止 trim 后的 `agentIds[0]` fallback；`official projection stream trims generated agentIds[0] fallback and taskId` 与 `official projection stream trims launch option agentId and taskId` 固定 executable payload，和 browser prototype runtime bootstrap 的 generated source-priority normalization 保持 parity。该证据只证明 official applet frontend controlled/local subscribe payload normalization，不证明真实 Desktop Host launch config、真实 Station SSE 网络恢复、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
- P2-03 official projection stream cursor normalization 也有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 现在对 explicit `afterEventSeq` 同时支持 number 与 trim 后可解析的 string；正数字符串会写入 subscribe payload，`certificationMode=product-window-e2e` 下的 `"0"` 字符串会保留为 zero cursor，其他非正数仍回退到 snapshot replay cursor 或省略。`official projection stream trims launch option cursor string` 与 `official projection stream launch options preserve product-window zero cursor string` 固定该路径，和 browser prototype runtime bootstrap 的 `normalizeAfterEventSeq()` parity 对齐。该证据只证明 official applet frontend controlled/local subscribe cursor normalization，不证明真实 Desktop Host launch config、真实 Station SSE 网络恢复、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
- P2-03 product-window zero cursor exception 也有 contract-backed controlled/local evidence：projection contract 现在显式记录 `zeroCursorPolicy=omit` 的默认策略，以及 `zeroCursorException` 中 `certificationMode=product-window-e2e` + `explicitZeroCursorPolicy=preserve` 的例外。official frontend、browser prototype runtime bootstrap 与 prototype Host subscribe payload helper 都读取该 generated policy；`testAppletBridgeProductWindowZeroCursorReachesHostSubscribe` 证明 product-window zero cursor 会进入 `atelier.events.subscribe` payload，而默认 zero cursor 仍按 omit 处理。该证据只证明 contract/codegen/prototype/official controlled parity，不证明真实 Desktop Host launch config、真实 Station SSE 网络恢复、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
- P2-03 projection stream cursor number policy 也有 contract-backed controlled/local evidence：projection contract 现在显式记录 `cursorNumberPolicy=safe_integer`，因为 Station `after_event_seq` / `next_event_seq` 真源类型为 int64，official frontend、browser prototype runtime bootstrap 与 prototype Host subscribe payload helper 都只接受 safe integer cursor。`official projection stream omits explicit decimal cursor and uses snapshot replay cursor`、`official projection stream omits unsafe integer cursor and uses snapshot replay cursor`、prototype `afterEventSeq: '1.5'` / `afterEventSeq: '9007199254740992'` unit cases 与 `omits decimal and unsafe replay cursor values from Host subscription payload` 固定 decimal / unsafe cursor fail-closed，不会把小数或 JS 不安全整数 cursor 投给 Host/Station。该证据只证明 contract/codegen/prototype/official controlled cursor normalization parity，不证明真实 Desktop Host launch config、真实 Station SSE 网络恢复、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
- 仍待落地：真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet live interrupt/resume E2E、DirectRun Provider execution 的真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、CLI CodingProvider 真实 Desktop worker execution、外部 provider billing 填充联调、rich artifact preview（sandbox markdown body / iframe / image） capability、safe text body fetch 的真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、Station SSE 生命周期管理和 Host 端到端联调；projection formal proto + JSON Schema/TS constants codegen、live mid-turn broker、artifact body store/retention、artifact `preview_hint` metadata projection、safe text body fetch capability、Provider/Gate typed plan 消费、provider runtime override、DirectRun persistence record、DirectRun no-session TaskRun/ExecutionStep marker、runtime preflight/recovery split、DirectRun model-provider service-level execution、DirectRun CLI typed Desktop handoff、DirectRun Budget/Policy pre-provider-call guard、DirectRun token/money usage ledger + provider pricing catalog snapshot + provider billing capture + pricing/billing reconciler、P1-10 Supervisor/Replan/Resume typed policy 和 blocking gate 恢复/复跑目前只完成 Station service-level / product-unit schema-codegen / contract gate 覆盖，真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E 仍未证明。

### 下一步落地顺序

按“先静态可验证，后真实联调”的顺序推进：

1. Projection contract guard：已建立 product-unit machine-readable contract（`apps/applets/atelier/contracts/atelier-projection.contract.json`）、formal projection proto（`model/domain/atelier/v1/projection.proto`）、generated JSON Schema（`atelier-projection.schema.generated.json`）、Station Go / Desktop TS proto 生成物、official / browser prototype generated TS constants（`projection.contract.generated.ts`）、contract gate（`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate`）和 TS runtime guard。
2. Bridge runtime hardening：已建立本地行为 gate（`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate`），覆盖 guard、去重、seq 防回放和错误状态；Desktop Host bridge/lifecycle 另由 `pnpm run atelier:desktop-bridge-gate` 覆盖 `<lynx-host>` Host command side-effect、manager-owned session lifecycle 与 Kernel-owned pause/resume，Desktop Host/page TypeScript wiring 由 `pnpm run atelier:desktop-typecheck-gate` 覆盖；browser prototype sync 由 `pnpm run atelier:prototype-gate` 覆盖 Vite build；official frontend packaging 由 `pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-build-gate` 覆盖 Lynx bundle build；Desktop static injection 由 `pnpm run applet:atelier-desktop-injection-gate` 覆盖 dist registry / manifest integrity / service binding；`pnpm run atelier:real-product-gates` 覆盖 Desktop Gateway 到 Station-bundled façade 的 workspace/replay product path、packaged `peers.atelier` normal Desktop product window workspace/subscribe/cancel/replay、cross-restart cursor recovery、human decision/live resume、Artifact/Gate projection、Host-rendered preview surface descriptor、metadata-only safe body fetch、blocking-gate `rerun_failed_node` Station orchestration provider recovery completion 与 sibling resolve variants；这些 gate 仍不覆盖任意网络故障矩阵、Host rich artifact renderer / Console Logs / attachment Host Storage 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
3. Desktop subscription lifecycle：已补 Desktop registry、重复订阅合并、unsubscribe/session cancel、有界 SSE 连接关闭、断线 backoff 和持久化 last-seq cursor；official Lynx applet 已具备基于显式 `agentId` / `agentIds[0]` 的默认 stream subscription 策略，并在 snapshot 已加载后对 event-stream subscribe failure 做 bounded retry（500/1500/5000ms），同时保留 degraded projection。剩余是真实端到端取消/恢复验证。
4. Station replay model：已从单一有限 raw event page 升级为 head / tail / projection anchor 聚合 replay，保证长任务中后段的 Artifact / Gate / Decision 不因首段分页丢失；snapshot 也会通过 `workspace.replay[taskId]` 暴露 `nextEventSeq / hasMore / checkpointId / checkpointEventSeq`。`FinishChatStep` 已写入 `TaskCheckpoint.StateJSON.atelierProjection`，读取侧已支持把该 materialized projection 折叠进 snapshot，并只叠加 checkpoint 之后的 event window；official Lynx applet 和 browser prototype bridge 已使用当前 task 的 `workspace.replay[taskId].nextEventSeq` 作为默认 `atelier.events.subscribe.afterEventSeq`，剩余是真实跨重启 cursor-based replay 验证。
5. Orchestration resume：`atelier.escalation.resolve` 已升级为结构化 `TASK_EVENT_TYPE_INTERRUPT_RESOLVED` 回写，避免把人工决策伪装成普通 turn；Station orchestration 已提供受控 `ResumeCollaborationTask` 入口，并用单测覆盖 `PAUSED` 恢复、running-node 冲突、`RUNNING` no-op 和 terminal reject。ResolveDecision 现在通过 orchestration 原子路径把 guarded resume 与 resolved outbox append 放进同一事务，避免不可恢复状态先污染 projection event；事务提交后才发布 realtime bus event 并按需启动执行。P0-08 的 `HumanDecisionRoute` 已在 Station service 层收敛：blocking gate 暂停时会写 `TASK_EVENT_TYPE_INTERRUPT_REQUESTED` + pending `human_decision`，payload 包含 `reason=gate_blocked`、`rollback_impact`、recommended `rerun_failed_node` 和 typed `HumanDecisionOption.action`；supervisor replan payload 同样包含通用 `reason` 与 typed `replan/continue/cancel` option action。resolve 前 Station 从 durable pending interrupt payload 读取 `EscalationPayload.reason` 与所选 `HumanDecisionOption.action`，标准化为 `human_decision_reason/action/transition`，再路由到既有 gate recovery / supervisor replan primitives；`policy + continue` hard deny 会被 Station 拒绝且不写 resolved event。`agent_interrupt_requests` 已接入 outbox writer，成为 pending/resolved/consumed interrupt lifecycle 的持久化索引，并已提供 `RequestCollaborationInterrupt` 给后续 runtime 在正确边界创建 pending request；`INTERRUPT_REQUESTED` / `INTERRUPT_RESOLVED` 的 snapshot 和 event projection 已闭环到 decision block / chosen option。恢复后的 Station-hosted turn 已能消费未使用的 `resume_payload_json`，并在成功 turn 后写回 consumed 标记。Live mid-turn resume broker 已在 Station service-level 接入：`OrchestrationService` 与 `TurnService` 共用 broker，只有 matching `task_id + interrupt_id` waiter 存在时才允许 `PAUSED + RUNNING node` 写 durable resolved event 并唤醒等待 turn，且 `resumed=false` 不启动新的 execution loop；无 waiter 仍拒绝且不写事件。Desktop 通用 orchestration proxy 也已暴露 `agent_collaboration_resume_task`，转发 `/sub-agent/agent/collaboration/resume`；它不进入 Atelier applet manifest / capability / projection contract。剩余是真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet live interrupt/resume E2E 验证。
6. Artifact / Gate production：orchestration 完成任务时已产出 GoalKeeper final summary artifact 与 GoalKeeper gate result；Desktop executor node result 已升级为 `SubmitCollaborationNodeResultRequest.artifacts / gates` 强类型提交，并由 Station outbox 持久化；`agent_task_artifacts` 已作为最小 Artifact evidence index 接入 outbox writer 和 migration；Artifact URI/checksum/refs policy 已在 node-result 入站与 outbox evidence index 两层强制；`agent_task_artifact_blobs` 已作为 Station-owned artifact body store 接入 outbox writer 和 migration，artifact 正文在写 outbox 前被抽取，projection/official UI 只展示 metadata；`atelier.artifact.body.fetch` 已提供显式 safe text fetch capability，official applet 不直接访问 backend URL 或本机路径，不加载 iframe/image/html；Gate result 已升级为 `TASK_EVENT_TYPE_GATE_RESULT`；`agent_task_gate_results` 已作为 gate result durable query index 接入 outbox writer 和 migration，覆盖正式 gate event 与 legacy `block_kind=gate_result` replay；Station 内部最小 `GateRunner` / `GateCheckExecutor` 已能执行 deterministic check 并写入 durable gate_result outbox；正式 `TaskGatePlan` proto / `agent_task_gate_plans` persistence 已补齐，gate result 可通过 `gate_plan_id` 回链；`TaskProviderPlan` / `TaskProviderSpec.agent_id/role` 已接入 CreateTask 与 `agent_task_provider_plans`，Station execution 已消费 persisted provider plan 覆盖 `TurnConfig.Provider/Model/Effort` 与 conversation metadata；DirectRun 已有 `agent_direct_runs` record、typed `TASK_SURFACE_DIRECT_RUN`、no-session `TaskRun`、`PENDING` Station-hosted `ExecutionStep`、durable `TASK_CREATED` marker event、runtime preflight 与 recovery split；DirectRun model-provider execution 已有 Station service-level runtime：专用 `executePendingDirectRun` 消费 pending marker，不创建 hidden `CollaborationSession`，provider call 前执行 Budget/Policy preflight，time budget exceeded、token usage 或 pricing/billed money usage 已达 cap、policy hard deny 会转为 paused + pending interrupt + failed blocking gate 且不调用 provider；provider call 成功会写 durable artifact、step completed、gate result、task status 与 `agent_task_budget_usages` token/money ledger + provider pricing catalog/legacy pricing snapshot；若 provider response 上报 billed money/source，Station 同账记录 provider billing evidence 并以 billed money 作为 effective `used_money`，`BudgetUsageReconciler` 可按 snapshot 校验 expected/actual money，并把 token usage without pricing/billing evidence 判为 missing pricing，provider failure 会写 failure artifact；CLI provider 会在 provider call 前转为 `direct_run_desktop_coding_provider_handoff` typed interrupt + blocking gate，payload 只投影 `cli_command_ref` 而不泄漏 raw CLI command；`TaskGateType` / `TaskGateCheckType` / `TaskGateEvaluatorSpec` / `TaskProviderSpec` 已接入 GatePlan 与 `GateRunner`，typed 字段优先，legacy string/JSON 只做 fallback；active GatePlan 静态调度路径已接入 node-result 事务；blocking gate 最小状态机会在失败时暂停 task、保留 `gate_blocked*` meta，并写 pending `human_decision` escalation payload；blocking 后恢复/复跑策略已在 Station service-level 落地，支持 `continue / rerun / accept / cancel`，并用 executor lease fence 防重复执行。剩余是 DirectRun Provider execution 真实 E2E、CLI CodingProvider 真实 Desktop worker execution、外部 provider billing 填充联调、rich artifact preview capability、真实 E2E gate recovery 验证。

7. Public typed node-result fixture：Station service 入口级 fixture 已覆盖 `SubmitCollaborationNodeResultRequest.artifacts / gates` 的公开 typed 提交路径，包含 desktop executor agent、active lease、durable outbox、artifact index、artifact blob redaction、gate result index、blocking pause 和 lease release；typed refs 的 self-ref、artifact URI ref 与 empty ref 会事务前拒绝且不污染 outbox。该项仍不等同真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。

8. Supervisor / Replan / Resume 前移：roadmap 已新增 B9.5，把 Supervisor Loop、Replan、Resume Anchor 前移到 B10 前，并把 Workspace/Sandbox 显式并入 B1；feature matrix 的 GAP-08~12 已更新为 typed-policy service-level done。`data-model.md` 现已补 `StationEvent` / `SupervisorEventPayload` / `TaskGraphDiffProposal` schema，明确内存 EventBus 只做 realtime fanout，durable `TaskEvent` 才是真源。Station proto 已新增 `TaskOrchestrationPolicy` / `TaskGraphParallelPolicy` / `SupervisorLoopKind` / `ReplanPolicyKind` / `ResumeAnchorPolicyKind`，并挂到 `TaskProviderPlan.orchestration_policy`；`CreateCollaborationTask` 会写入 typed policy 并派生当前 meta index，parallel engine 必须有 `provider_plan.synthesizer_agent_id` 作为 integrator identity，无 integrator 时只能走 `serial_only`。`RunCollaborationSupervisorTick` 已提供 service-level runtime tick：从 durable task state 发现 workspace conflict / max rounds / max fix loops / budget 卡点后，暂停 task 并写 `supervisor_replan` pending interrupt，payload 带 `resume_anchor_checkpoint_id` / `resume_anchor_event_seq` 与 versioned `atelier.task_graph_diff/v0` proposal（affected / retained nodes + proposed action），由既有 projection decision block 呈现；`RunCollaborationSupervisorSweep` 已提供 bounded service-level scheduler sweep，可扫描 RUNNING/PAUSED tasks 并复用 tick 的 transactional replan request / duplicate fence；`JobKindCollaborationSupervisor` 已接入 Station scheduler 并直接调用 orchestration service，不走 HTTP 自调；Goal Owner 在 decision block 选择 `replan` 后，Station interrupt resolution 会校验 pending `supervisor_replan` 并事务内 apply `atelier.task_graph_diff/v0`，把 affected nodes 重置为 pending、保留 retained nodes。Atelier `createFromGoal` 只传 typed provider plan 和 integrator identity，不暴露 `atelier.supervisor.*` / `atelier.replan.*` / `atelier.integrator.*` method。剩余是真实运行时 E2E、workspace conflict E2E、跨重启 resume E2E 验证。

DirectRun / RunPicker 当前同步口径：browser prototype 与 official applet 都只把「⚡ 直连模型」和「Agents flow」表达为 `createFromGoal` 的 Station-owned intent；model 分支提交 `run.kind=model / run.model` 且不携带 `run.flowId`，agents 分支提交 `run.kind=agents / run.agentIds` 与可选 `run.flowId`；`atelier.message.send` 保持 text-only，不携带 message-level run intent。browser prototype 的 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 也固定 RunPicker 只写 Station-owned intent，并拒绝 `runtime.invokeProvider`、`provider.invoke`、`model.run`、`runModel`、`cli.execute`、`executeCli` 出现在 prototype page source。official `RunTargetSelector` 也显式披露 run target selection 只写入 Station-owned DirectRun / agents intent，不 invoke provider、不 run model、不编排 agents、不 execute CLI，且本地 gate 不证明真实 provider/agents E2E。Contract JSON 声明 `allowedRunKinds=[agents,model]`、`defaultRunKind=agents` 与 `directRunIntent`，codegen 导出 `ATELIER_RUN_TARGET_KINDS` / `AtelierRunTargetKind` / `ATELIER_DEFAULT_RUN_TARGET_KIND`；official client、official controller 默认 run kind、official `RunTargetSelector`、browser prototype runtime 与 browser `RunPicker` 均消费 generated taxonomy/default，不维护本地 `model|agents` union/list，也不把 `allowedRunKinds` 数组顺序当作默认值语义；Station `validateAtelierRunTarget` 要求 `run.kind=model` 必须带 `run.model`、不得带 `run.flowId`，并把 typed provider plan source 标为 `atelier.direct_run.intent`；Station execution 会从 `agent_task_provider_plans.plan_json` 读取 provider spec 覆盖 `TurnConfig.Provider/Model/Effort` 与 conversation metadata；Station `agent_direct_runs` 会保存 `input_snapshot_json / budget_ref / policy_ref / trace_id`；Station 已用 `TASK_SURFACE_DIRECT_RUN` 表达 no-session surface，并写入 `ConversationID=""` 的 `TaskRun`、`PENDING` 的 Station-hosted `ExecutionStep` 与 durable `TASK_CREATED` marker event；runtime preflight fail-closed 校验 provider/model/budget/policy/trace refs 与 pending step，recovery 会把 DirectRun 路由到专用 no-session runtime 而不是普通 collaboration execution。Station service-level DirectRun model-provider execution 已能调用内部 provider executor，并在 provider call 前执行 time budget / token usage / pricing/billed money usage / policy hard deny guard，把阻断写成 pending interrupt + failed blocking gate；成功会写 durable artifact、step/task event、gate result 与 `agent_task_budget_usages` token/money ledger + provider pricing catalog/legacy pricing snapshot；provider response 可上报 billed money/source，Station 会记录 estimated money 与 billed actual 并让 reconciler 对账。失败会写 failure artifact。CLI provider 会在 provider call 前转为 typed Desktop runtime handoff interrupt，记录 `adapter=desktop_coding_provider`、`executor_kind=EXECUTOR_KIND_DESKTOP_DEVICE`、`requested_capabilities=[cli]` 与 `cli_command_ref=agent_provider.cli_command`，不调用 Station provider executor，也不投影 raw CLI command。Applet 不获得 `invoke/execute/run/shell/file` 或 Provider 调用权限。CLI CodingProvider 真实 Desktop worker execution、真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、外部 provider billing 填充联调与真实 provider/live stream 验证仍未落。

Method intent metadata 当前同步口径：`atelier-projection.contract.json` 为所有 applet-visible methods 声明 `methodIntents`，每个 method 都有 `intentOwner=station|desktop_host`、`intentKind`、`sideEffectClass=none|host_ui|station_transaction` 与 `executionForbidden=true`。formal `AtelierProjectionContract` proto 也已暴露 `repeated AtelierAppletMethodIntent method_intents = 7`，Go/TS proto 生成物同步提供 `MethodIntents` / `AtelierAppletMethodIntent` / `executionForbidden`，避免 formal proto consumer 只能看到裸 method 列表。`generate-atelier-projection-contract.mjs` 会校验并导出 `ATELIER_METHOD_INTENTS`，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 会强制 methodIntents 覆盖全部 methods、拒绝未知 owner/side-effect，并固定 `atelier.project.createFromGoal=station_transaction`、`atelier.artifact.preview.open=desktop_host/host_ui`、`atelier.provider.capabilities=none`。这只是 contract guard，不新增 applet 执行权限。

Work/Code/Design preset 当前同步口径：`atelier.project.createFromGoal` 的 contract payload 已声明 `allowedIntentPresets=[work,code,design]` 与 `intentPresetMapping`，`workbenchSurface.defaultTaskIntentPreset=work` 生成 `ATELIER_DEFAULT_TASK_INTENT_PRESET`，Station 会把 applet 提交的 `intentPreset` fail-closed 归一化为 `intent_preset / provider_strategy_preset / gate_plan_preset` meta 并投影到 task；official Lynx applet 的新建任务区和 browser prototype 的 Work/Code/Design toggle 都只提交这个声明式 preset。Code 映射 `coding_provider_preferred + lint_typecheck_build`，Design 映射 `design_review_preferred + prototype_visual_review`，Work 映射 `station_generalist_research + plan_review_evidence`。official Lynx applet 与 browser prototype toggle 都已改为消费 generated `ATELIER_TASK_INTENT_PRESETS`；official client 的 `AtelierIntentPreset` 也由 generated preset tuple 派生，official label map 用 `Record<AtelierIntentPreset,...>` type-check，official create fallback 与 controller 初始值都消费 generated `ATELIER_DEFAULT_TASK_INTENT_PRESET`，不再本地写死 `work` 默认值；browser prototype toggle 用 `TaskIntentPreset = (typeof ATELIER_TASK_INTENT_PRESETS)[number]` type-check，browser prototype runtime 的 `CreateProjectFromGoalInput.intentPreset` 也通过 `IntentPreset = (typeof ATELIER_TASK_INTENT_PRESETS)[number]` 派生，runtime metadata fallback 消费 generated default，避免两侧重新维护本地 `['work','code','design']` taxonomy 或默认 preset literal；browser prototype domain types 也由 generated workbench taxonomy 派生 `TaskIntentPreset / ArtifactKind / ArtifactBodyKind / GateStatus / GateCheckStatus / TodoStatus`，不再维护本地 artifact/gate/todo union；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 分别固定 official/browser toggle 只能写声明式 preset，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 prototype type-level taxonomy guard，并拒绝 `atelier.ide.mode`、`ide.mode.switch`、`workspace.mode.switch`、`runtime.mode.switch`、`provider.runtime.override` 出现在 page source。这不切真实 IDE mode，不触发 provider invoke，不切 workspace/runtime，不产出 gate/artifact。

`workbenchSurface.controlledEvidence` 当前把 workbench taxonomy 提升为机器可审计 metadata：它固定 `readiness=controlled_local_upstream`、`evidenceClass=CONTROLLED_LOCAL_UPSTREAM`、`surfaceRole=applet_projection_surface`、`sourceOfTruth=station_projection_contract`、Work/Code/Design preset、todo/context file groups、organizer modes `folders/flat-list/kanban/dag` 与 ready state、artifact/artifact body/gate status taxonomy，并记录 `generatedTaxonomyProven=true`、`officialGeneratedConsumptionProven=true`、`prototypeGeneratedConsumptionProven=true`、`readyOrganizerModesProven=true`、`disabledOrganizerModesExecutionForbidden=true`、`artifactBodyKindAllowlistFeedsBodyFetch=true`、`gateStatusTaxonomyGenerated=true`、`appletOrganizerExecutionExposed=false`、`appletArtifactBodyKindOverrideExposed=false`、`realKanbanDagRuntimeProven=false`、`realArtifactRendererRuntimeProven=false` 与 `realHostStationAppletE2EProven=false`。该证据只证明 official/prototype 消费 generated workbench taxonomy、ready/disabled organizer boundary、artifact body kind allowlist 与 gate status taxonomy；不证明 kanban/DAG runtime、真实 artifact renderer runtime 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

UNSYNCED：New task 的目标产品语义仍是先创建 `Project{state:draft}`，首条用户消息再触发契约化；当前 official Lynx applet 与 browser prototype 都仍使用 `atelier.project.createFromGoal` create-from-goal shortcut，但 browser prototype 的 New task 已改为打开 goal composer，提交用户输入后才走 create-from-goal controlled shortcut，不再传入固定 goal `新任务`。这仍不是正式 draft Project E2E。落地 draft Project flow 前，不能把 New task shortcut 反推为最终 `Project: (none)->draft` 行为。

Artifact preview 当前同步口径：`packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier/` 的 browser prototype 已与 official projection boundary 对齐为 metadata-only preview，不再保留 markdown/web/image/diff raw rich render branch，也不再显示 `Prototype-only rich preview` banner；browser prototype 的 Console Logs 面板只读取 mock `Artifact.logs`，并显示 `Prototype-only mock logs: real Run runtime stream is not wired.`；`atelier-bridge-runtime-gate` 固定该 disclosure 且禁止 `runtime.logs.subscribe` / `atelier.logs.subscribe` / `console.logs.subscribe` 进入 browser prototype，不能把 mock logs 反推为真实 Run Console Logs stream。同时 browser prototype 的 right preview 已补 “Host safe text capability” 区块，通过 runtime `fetchArtifactBody` 表达 official 当前 `atelier.artifact.body.fetch` 行为；该 capability 只在 Station-projected `Artifact.bodyRef` 存在时可用，缺失时显示 `Station-projected bodyRef missing`，prototype 不再合成本地 `artifact://.../body`。official Lynx applet 与 Station projection 当前按 P2-07 只完成 `preview_hint` + body metadata + `previewTarget` metadata-only descriptor + explicit safe text body fetch，并且 official / browser prototype projection guard 都会拒绝 raw `url/src`、正文、html/diff/patch/iframe 泄漏；top-level `Artifact.bodyRef` 与 `previewTarget.bodyRef` 都只接受 canonical `artifact://<task_id>/<artifact_id>/body`，拒绝 `file://` / `http(s)://` / segment 含空白或 malformed `artifact://...` body ref；`previewTarget` 一旦存在，必须是完整 sandbox manifest tuple：`mode=sandbox_manifest`、`sandboxRef=atelier-sandbox://...`、`bodyRef=artifact://<task_id>/<artifact_id>/body`，缺任一字段都会被 projection guard 拒绝；browser prototype 的 Host sandbox preview capability 也只在 Station-projected `previewTarget.sandboxRef/bodyRef` 存在时可用，缺失时显示 `Station-projected previewTarget missing`，不再合成本地 `atelier-sandbox://.../preview`。official 与 browser prototype 都不加载 iframe / image / external URL，也不渲染 html。后续 sandbox rich preview runtime 落地时需要同步 prototype 与 official behavior。
9. Official applet bundle：`apps/applets/atelier` 已 scaffold 为 official applet product unit，frontend 可构建 `main.lynx.bundle`，并已接入 `GET /v1/workspace` service binding 的 Lynx projection UI。当前 official frontend 已从最小 summary 升级为 controlled single-column projection surface：任务 organizer、中央 projection stream、Project Health projection + TaskGraph projection first / legacy Todo fallback + Context / Artifacts / Artifact Preview / Gates 以单栏 section 堆叠；顶栏展示 budget、run target 与 replay metadata，流块能分型展示 decision / negotiation / artifact 摘要。official projection 面板只读展示 `workspace.projects[]` 的 Project Health projection（completion、open blockers、residual risks、milestones、memory candidates、policy rules、defect proposals），并显式声明 applet 不 accept、不 waive、不 mutate project state；blocker / risk / milestone / memory candidate / policy rule / defect 紧凑列表折叠时显示剩余 Station projection 数量。随后按 `workspace.projects[].taskGraph` 展示 Station-owned TaskGraph 节点、角色与 evidence refs，没有 project projection 时才回退 legacy Todo；紧凑面板最多展开前 5 个 TaskGraph 节点，并显式显示剩余 projected node 数量；legacy Todo fallback 最多展开前 5 条，并显式显示剩余 legacy todo projection 数量，避免静默隐藏 Station 投影；面板文案明确 applet 不调度、不执行、不 replan 节点，并由 `atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定。官方 Lynx UI 已显式覆盖 `loading / empty / reconciling / degraded / disconnected / auth-denied / error / ready`：`reconciling` 表示 snapshot 已加载但 projection stream 正在连接，`degraded` 表示保留最后有效 projection 但 replay/event stream 覆盖不完整，`disconnected` 和 `auth-denied` 不再退化为通用 Error；该派生已抽为 `deriveAtelierViewStatus` pure helper，并由 `atelier:official-frontend-gate` 执行 controlled state matrix。official event-stream subscribe 已有 bounded retry：有 snapshot 时对 disconnected/generic subscribe failure 依次 500/1500/5000ms retry，auth-denied / invalid-projection / 无 snapshot / 超过次数不 retry，成功后回到 `live`；该策略由 `nextAtelierEventStreamRetryDelayMs` pure helper 和 official gate retry matrix 固定。中央 composer 上方已补 Artifacts 托盘，点击托盘卡片会复用右侧只读 metadata preview，不加载 iframe、图片或外部 URL。negotiation 流块默认折叠，只显示 summary / agent count / 收敛状态；展开后展示 consensus 与 voices，voices 使用 role avatar marker、role-colored lane、stance chip、text 与 evidenceRef，并把无 evidence 的 objection 显示为「疑虑」而非阻断性反对。Gates 面板已能只读展示 summary、check、artifact evidence，不加载外部 URL 或 raw artifact body；official guard 会拒绝 projection 中的 `markdown/content/body/html/diff/patch/url/src` 泄漏字段，正文只允许通过 `atelier.artifact.body.fetch` service binding 显式获取 safe text。P2-08 Skills / 斜杠命令能力已按 discovery-only 同步：Station 暴露 `atelier.provider.capabilities`，official Lynx applet 通过 `/v1/provider/capabilities` service binding 获取，browser prototype 通过 local bridge runtime 模拟，official 与 browser prototype 都只展示 `scope=station-provider/readOnly=true` 的 capability descriptor，并在点击时把 `/implement`、`/review`、`/test` 等 slash command 插入 composer；不暴露 `atelier.provider.invoke`、`skills.invoke`、`execute` 或 `run`。official frontend 也已消费 `atelier.projection.event`，通过本地 reducer 应用 snapshot / task / stream / decision / artifact / gate / context / todo patch，并具备 event 去重与同 scope 过期 seq 拒绝；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 已覆盖 typed context/todo patch、rich stream guard、negotiation folded disclosure + role/stance rendering、Project Health read-only panel + overflow disclosure、TaskGraph read-only panel + overflow disclosure、legacy Todo fallback overflow disclosure、gate detail guard/rendering、artifact tray / preview guard、页面 section、locale key、controlled 状态矩阵与 retry 矩阵、new task create-from-goal run target intent、task lifecycle、purge 二次确认、composer/decision client-controller wiring、artifact metadata + safe text fetch guard、raw artifact reference rejection 和 provider capability discovery panel。`pnpm applets:build` 会同步到 `apps/desktop/applets-dist/peers.atelier` 并写入真实 dist manifest integrity；`pnpm run applet:atelier-desktop-injection-gate` 会验证 Desktop index 注入、dist manifest、bundle integrity、Atelier service binding、service manifest route、stationBundled dependency、source/desktop manifest 一致性，并从 `atelier-projection.contract.json` 校验当前全部 Atelier method permissions，同时拒绝 provider invoke / memory write / rerun / artifact/gate produce / execute/run/shell/file 等 forbidden permissions。P3-09 已补 Station official façade：`official_applet_atelier` mount `/applets/atelier/`，`/applets/atelier/v1` 映射 workspace/projects/messages/escalations/task lifecycle HTTP contract，并委托 Station-owned `AtelierProjectionService`；official frontend 的 workspace/create/message/escalation/task lifecycle 已切到 `sdk.network.request({ service:'atelier', path:'/v1/...' })` service binding 产品路径，provider discovery / feedback / artifact body fetch 已切到 service binding；workspace open 保持 Host-local capability；events subscribe 保持 Host event broker + Station stream；injection gate、official frontend gate 与 projection contract gate 会一起校验 Station main 注册、façade mapping、manifest allowedPaths、dist manifest、frontend service path 与 forbidden invoke。剩余是更复杂的原型交互迁移（如 web iframe/Console Logs 级别的真实产物运行面板、图片附件），以及 Desktop Host + Station + applet 端到端验证。

Event subscription controlled evidence metadata 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`eventSubscription.controlledEvidence` 将 `atelier.projection.event`、`atelier.events.subscribe`、agent/task source priority、`workspace.replay[taskId].nextEventSeq` cursor source 与 `zeroCursorPolicy=omit` 绑定到 `atelier:official-frontend-gate` / `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 及其 evidence JSON；metadata 固定 `hostEventBridgeRequired=true`、`missingHostEventBridgeFailsClosed=true`、`subscriptionRejectionTypedRecoveryProven=true`、`eventVsSnapshotFreshnessProven=true`、`releaseBeforeRejectCleanupProven=true`、`boundedRetryMatrixProven=true`，并保留 `realStationSseFailureMatrixProven=false`、`realCrossRestartE2EProven=false`、`realHostStationAppletE2EProven=false`。browser prototype `appletBridge` 还会在 `host.onEvent()` 返回 malformed unsubscribe cleanup 时 fail-closed：投递 typed `atelier.projection.subscription-rejected`、请求 Host topic unsubscribe，并且不继续启动 `events.subscribe` 或 canonical Station projection subscribe。该证据只证明 official/prototype controlled subscription source priority、cursor policy、bridge fail-closed 与 typed recovery 边界，不证明真实 Station SSE 任意失败矩阵、真实 cross-restart E2E 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Task lifecycle controlled evidence metadata 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`taskLifecycle.controlledEvidence` 将 `status` / `workbench_lifecycle` / `orthogonalTo=execution_state`、`active/archived/deleted`、`purgeRequiresStatus=deleted`、forbidden execution status values `running/succeeded/failed/paused/blocked` 绑定到 `atelier:official-frontend-gate` / `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 及其 evidence JSON；metadata 固定 `setStatusMethod=atelier.task.setStatus`、`purgeMethod=atelier.task.purge`、`setStatusPayloadAllowedStatusMatchesStates=true`、`purgePayloadRequiresDeletedStatus=true`、`statusOrthogonalToExecutionState=true`、`lifecycleMenuExecutionActionsForbidden=true`、`purgeBypassForbidden=true`，并保留 `realStationTaskLifecycleE2EProven=false`、`realPurgeE2EProven=false`、`realExecutionStateTransitionProven=false` 与 `realHostStationAppletE2EProven=false`。Browser prototype 的 `setTaskStatus` / `purgeTask` async success 和 failure 都由 operation + task + target status request key 拥有；late success / late failure 会被 stale guard 拒绝，当前 failure 只复用 bridge recovery status，不暴露 execution/provider/runtime/memory/input_snapshot 能力。`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 与 `atelier:projection-contract-gate` 同时拒绝 malformed TaskHost lifecycle indentation 回流，保证 source hygiene 不掩盖 lifecycle ownership 逻辑。`atelier:task-lifecycle-controlled-gate` 进一步证明 Station `SetTaskStatus` 持久化 workbench lifecycle 时不改变 execution status，`PurgeTask` 通过 public service 拒绝 non-deleted task，并在 deleted 后清理 task-owned durable indexes。该证据只证明 official/prototype controlled lifecycle taxonomy、Station service-level lifecycle persistence、purge 前置条件与 execution-state 隔离边界，不证明真实 Station task lifecycle E2E、真实 purge E2E、真实 execution state transition、真实 product-window task menu interaction、真实 cross-device lifecycle synchronization 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official negotiation disclosure 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official `NegoDisclosure` 已同步 browser prototype 的折叠交互，默认只显示 summary / agent count / 收敛状态，点击后才展开 consensus 与 voices；展开态不再是纯文本列表，`NegoVoiceList` 会渲染 role avatar marker、role-colored visual lane、stance chip，并继续把无 evidence 的 objection 降级为 `atelier.nego.concern`。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定默认 collapsed、本地 expand state、role/stance visual anchors、`atelier.nego.converged` / `atelier.nego.pending` 文案和 hidden voices disclosure。该证据只证明 official 本地 projection UI parity，不证明真实 multi-agent negotiation runtime 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official / browser TaskGraph parallel policy disclosure 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official `TaskGraphPanel` 与 browser prototype `TaskGraphProjectionPanel` 现在都只读展示 `workspace.projects[].taskGraph.parallelPolicy`，并以 `rootTaskIds` 与 `edges` 紧凑列表展示 Station-projected TaskGraph roots / dependency edges；TaskGraph node row 也显示 node-level `artifactIds` / `gateIds` 只读 refs，而不只显示 evidence count。折叠时显示剩余 root / edge / node / node evidence ref projection 数量
，避免已通过 ingress guard 的 TaskGraph metadata 被 UI 静默隐藏。当 policy 为 `integrator_required` 时，UI 明确披露 integrator identity 与 merge execution 仍归 Station，Applet 只投影策略，不调度、不执行、不 replan，也不在 applet 内执行合并。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 official page/locale anchors，并截取 official `TaskGraphPanel` source，拒绝 `taskGraph.schedule`、`taskGraph.execute`、`taskGraph.replan`、`taskGraph.diff.apply`、`integrator.merge.execute` 出现在 panel source；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official/prototype panel anchors 与 official source guard anchor；`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 也固定 browser prototype TaskGraph 只读 Station projection boundary，并拒绝相同调度/执行/replan/merge capability 出现在 prototype page。该证据只证明 TaskGraph root/edge/node-evidence/parallel/integrator policy visibility parity，也证明 official/browser prototype 都不暴露调度/执行/replan capability；不证明真实 Integrator merge runtime、TaskGraph production/runtime、artifact/gate production、Station projection production 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Artifact bodyRef response guard 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`artifactPreview.bodyRefShape` 是 canonical `artifact://<task>/<artifact>/body` 的 contract source-of-truth，并生成 `ATELIER_ARTIFACT_BODY_REF_SHAPE` 供 official client / official projection guard / browser applet-sdk bridge / browser prototype projection guard 消费；browser applet-sdk `appletBridge` 的 non-snapshot Host response guard 与 official/prototype projection guard 对齐，task/artifact segment 不允许空白字符；`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 覆盖 body fetch 与 preview open response 中含空白 segment 的 malformed bodyRef fail-closed。该证据只证明 adapter response guard，不证明真实 artifact body fetch、Host sandbox renderer 或完整 E2E。

Official legacy Todo fallback overflow 当前有 controlled/local evidence：当 `workspace.projects[]` 不存在或无法匹配当前任务时，official frontend 回退到 legacy Todo projection；该 fallback 仍只读展示 Station todo projection，紧凑列表折叠时显示剩余 legacy todo projection 数量，并由 `atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 `visibleTodos`、`hiddenTodoCount` 与 `atelier.todos.moreProjected` 文案。该证据只证明 fallback UI 不静默隐藏 todo projection，不证明真实 TaskGraph production/runtime、legacy Todo 真源迁移或真实 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official Project Health projection 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 与 browser prototype 的右栏在 TaskGraph 前展示 Project Health projection，包含 completion predicates、goal owner signoff、open blockers、residual risks、milestones、memory candidates、policy rules 与 defect proposals；blocker / risk / milestone / memory candidate / policy rule / defect 紧凑列表折叠时显示剩余 Station projection 数量。Project Health compact item limit、milestone refs limit、TaskGraph root/edge/node/node-ref limit、legacy Todo fallback limit、provider capability list、negotiation voices、diff paths、context file/other refs、side-panel artifacts、artifact paths、safe text preview lines、gate list/check/artifact-ref limits 已由 `projectSurface.projectionDisplayLimits` 生成 `ATELIER_PROJECTION_DISPLAY_LIMITS`；official 与 browser prototype 均消费适用的 generated descriptor，不再在各自面板本地维护 `3/4/5/2/80` display limits；browser prototype TaskGraph nodes、provider capability list 与 Nego voices 也与 official 一样按 generated limit 披露剩余 Station projection 数量。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 `ProjectHealthPanel`、`ProjectionItemList`、只读文案、locale 与 memory/policy/defect overflow anchors，并截取 official `ProjectHealthPanel` source，拒绝 `project.health.accept`、`project.health.waive`、`project.state.mutate`、`acceptancePredicate.evaluate`、`policy.engine.run`、`defect.lifecycle.mutate` 出现在 panel source；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official source guard anchor、browser prototype 的 `ProjectHealthProjectionPanel`、只读文案、overflow counters 与 generated display-limit consumption，并拒绝 provider/voice/context/artifact/gate/safe-text/diff path limits 回退为本地数字；`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 也固定 browser prototype Project Health 只读 Station projection boundary，并拒绝相同项目变更/evaluator capability 出现在 prototype page。该证据只证明 applet 不静默隐藏已投影 metadata，也证明 official/browser prototype 都不暴露项目变更/evaluator capability；不证明完整 Project/Milestone runtime、AcceptancePredicate DSL、memory write、Policy engine、Defect governance lifecycle 或真实 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official / browser milestone refs disclosure 当前有 controlled/local evidence：Project Health milestone 行不再只显示 predicate/blocker 数量，official frontend 与 browser prototype 都会紧凑展示 milestone-level `acceptancePredicateIds` 与 `openBlockers` refs，并显示剩余 predicate/blocker ref 数量；这仍是 Station-owned Milestone projection 的只读披露，不授予 applet AcceptancePredicate evaluator、blocker lifecycle、accept/waive/mutate 或 ProjectStateMachine 执行权。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 official `visiblePredicateIds` / `visibleMilestoneBlockerRefs` 与 locale anchors，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 browser prototype anchors。该证据只证明 milestone refs visibility parity，不证明完整 Milestone runtime、AcceptancePredicate DSL 或真实 Host+Station+applet E2E。
