`projectSurface.controlledEvidence` 当前把 Project/TaskGraph/Memory/Policy/Defect projection taxonomy 提升为机器可审计 metadata：它固定 `readiness=controlled_local_upstream`、`evidenceClass=CONTROLLED_LOCAL_UPSTREAM`、`surfaceRole=applet_read_only_projection_taxonomy`、`sourceOfTruth=station_projection_contract`、blocker/risk/dependency/project/milestone/taskGraph/memory/policy/defect enums、`projectionDisplayLimits`、display surfaces `project_health/task_graph`，并记录 `generatedTaxonomyProven=true`、`officialProjectionGuardProven=true`、`prototypeProjectionGuardProven=true`、`displayLimitConsumptionProven=true`、`projectHealthReadOnlyBoundaryProven=true`、`taskGraphReadOnlyBoundaryProven=true`、`memoryCandidateProjectionGuardProven=true`、`policyDefectProjectionGuardProven=true`、`appletProjectMutationExposed=false`、`appletTaskGraphMutationExposed=false`、`appletMemoryWriteExposed=false`、`appletPolicyOverrideExposed=false`、`appletDefectLifecycleMutationExposed=false`、`realProjectStateMachineProven=false`、`realTaskGraphProductionRuntimeProven=false`、`realMemoryWriteE2EProven=false`、`realPolicyEngineProven=false`、`realDefectGovernanceLifecycleProven=false` 与 `realHostStationAppletE2EProven=false`。该证据只证明 official/prototype projection guard、generated display-limit consumption、Project Health/TaskGraph read-only boundary 与 malformed projection rejection；不证明 ProjectStateMachine、TaskGraph production runtime、memory write、Policy engine、Defect lifecycle 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official artifact projection overflow 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 的 Artifacts 托盘与右侧 Artifacts 面板只展示 metadata projection，不加载 iframe、图片、外部 URL 或 raw body；紧凑列表折叠 artifact 时会显示剩余 Station artifact projection 数量，artifact preview 中的 paths 也会显示剩余 Station artifact path ref 数量，并由 `atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 `hiddenArtifactCount`、`hiddenArtifactPathCount`、`atelier.artifact.moreProjected`、`atelier.artifact.morePaths` 和 metadata-only 文案。artifact preview 还显式显示 `Preview boundary`，声明 markdown/web/image/diff 都保持 metadata-only，official rendering 只限 safe text fetch 与 Host sandbox manifest intent；browser prototype `preview.tsx` 也固定为 `ArtifactMetadataPreview`，只展示 metadata-only preview、projected paths、prototype-only mock logs disclosure、Host safe text fetch 与 Host sandbox preview intent，不再包含 raw rich render branch 或旧 rich preview notice component。该证据只证明 official UI 不静默隐藏已投影 artifact/path metadata，且 browser prototype 与 official metadata-only artifact preview boundary 对齐；不证明 rich sandbox renderer、Console Logs 真实 runtime stream 或 artifact body/render E2E。

Official / prototype context projection overflow 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 的 Context 面板仍只读展示 Station projection 的 token usage 与 context file refs；面板显式显示 `atelier.context.readOnly`，说明它不执行 Workspace file discovery，也不写入 Run input_snapshot；browser prototype `ContextPanel` 同步显示 `Read-only Station context projection`，明确没有 Workspace file discovery、没有 Run input_snapshot write、也没有 Host+Station+applet E2E proof。official Context 现在与 browser prototype 的 Files / Other 信息结构对齐，分别派生 `visibleFileRefs` / `visibleOtherRefs` 与 `hiddenFileRefCount` / `hiddenOtherRefCount`，紧凑折叠时分别显示剩余 Station context file ref 与 other context ref 数量；browser prototype 的 `ContextFile.group` 类型与 Context tabs 也从 generated `ATELIER_CONTEXT_FILE_GROUPS` / `ContextFileGroup` 派生，不再维护本地 `files` / `other` union/list；默认选中 tab 也由 generated `ATELIER_DEFAULT_CONTEXT_FILE_GROUP` 驱动，不再用 `ATELIER_CONTEXT_FILE_GROUPS[0]` 从数组顺序推导；official 与 prototype projection guard 同时要求 `usedPct` 为 `0..100`。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 `ContextRefGroup`、Files/Other grouped refs、`atelier.context.moreFiles`、`atelier.context.moreOther` 与 read-only 文案，并截取 official `ContextPanel` source，拒绝 `workspace.files.discover`、`WorkspaceFileDiscovery`、`context.files.refresh`、`input_snapshot.write`、`inputSnapshot.write` 出现在 panel source；`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 固定 malformed context usage snapshot matrix、browser prototype Context source-level forbidden capability 与 generated Context group selector/type anchors；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 grouped UI、bounded guard、generated Context group default/consumer anchors 与 official/browser ownership disclosure anchors。该证据只证明 UI 不静默隐藏已投影 context metadata 且披露 context ownership boundary，也证明 official/browser Context panel 不暴露 workspace discovery/input snapshot writes；不证明真实 Workspace file discovery、Run input snapshot 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official gate projection overflow 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 的 Gates 面板只读展示 Station gate projection summary、checks 与 artifact evidence refs，不执行 gate、不读取 raw artifact body；紧凑列表折叠 gate、check 或 artifact evidence ref 时会显示剩余数量，并由 `atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 `hiddenGateCount`、`hiddenCheckCount`、`hiddenGateArtifactCount` 与 `atelier.gate.moreProjected/moreChecks/moreArtifacts` 文案。`GatePanel` success tone 也已从旧 `pass` 字符串收敛到 generated workbench taxonomy 中的 canonical `passed`，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 `GATE_STATUS_PASSED` 由 `ATELIER_GATE_STATUSES` type-check，且禁止 `gate.status === 'pass'` 回流。formal `AtelierArtifactProjection` proto 也已 reserve raw `url/src` 字段号与字段名，Go/TS proto 生成物不再暴露 `Url/Src`，并新增 `AtelierArtifactPreviewTargetProjection preview_target = 13` 来表达 metadata-only sandbox manifest descriptor；Station runtime `AtelierArtifactRef` 与 event mapper 不再暴露或复制 `markdown/url/src/iframe` raw fields，redaction list 与 JSON artifact contract 的 forbidden body fields 对齐；artifact evidence 只能以 ids/body refs/previewTarget sandbox metadata 投影。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 也截取 official `GatePanel` source，拒绝 `gate.execute`、`check.execute`、`gate.rerun`、`gate.accept`、`gateResult.submit`、`gate.produce`、`artifact.produce` 出现在 panel source；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定该 official source guard anchor。该证据只证明 official UI 不静默隐藏 gate evidence projection，canonical 渲染 Station gate status，formal proto 与 Station runtime projection 不暴露 raw artifact refs，也不暴露 GateRunner execution/result submission capability；不证明真实 GateRunner 调度、真实验收 E2E 或 artifact render E2E。

Official Project / TaskGraph projection schema 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 与 browser prototype 的 Project projection guard 不再只做浅层 string 检查；`Project.state in {draft,contracted,executing,blocked,verifying,awaiting_owner_signoff,accepted,escalated}`、`Milestone.state in {planned,active,blocked,replanning,accepted,abandoned}`、TaskGraph node query-index state `in {todo,running,done}`、`Blocker.severity/state`、`ResidualRisk.state`、`DependencyEdge.type` 与 `TaskGraph.parallelPolicy` 会按 allowlist 校验，project/task graph IDs、workspaceRef、owner、evidenceRef、reason、TaskGraph refs 等最小渲染引用也必须非空。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 都加入 malformed project snapshot matrix，覆盖 bad project state、bad milestone state、bad task graph node state、bad blocker severity、bad residual risk state、bad dependency edge type 与 bad parallel policy；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official/prototype enum helper 与 gate fixtures。该证据只证明 projection ingress 会拒绝 malformed Project / TaskGraph / Blocker envelopes，不证明完整 ProjectStateMachine、AcceptancePredicate、TaskGraph business schema 或真实 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official / prototype MemoryCandidate projection schema 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 与 browser prototype 的 Project projection guard 会校验 `MemoryCandidate.type in {success_pattern,failure_cause,project_rule,domain_rule,arch_decision,workflow_improvement}`、`MemoryCandidate.scope in {user,project,domain}` 与 `MemoryCandidate.feeds[] in {planner,risk,verifier}`，并要求 memory candidate id/content 非空；evidenceRefs 仍保持 string-array 形状以兼容缺少 source block id 的历史 projection。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 都加入 malformed MemoryCandidate snapshot matrix，覆盖 bad type、empty content、bad scope 与 bad feed；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official/prototype enum helper 与 gate fixtures。该证据只证明 projection ingress 会拒绝 malformed MemoryCandidate envelopes，不证明真实 Planner/Risk/Verifier 反哺消费、长期记忆写入或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official Policy / Defect projection schema 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 与 browser prototype 的 Project projection guard 会继续深入校验 `policy` 与 `defects`，`PolicyRule.scope in {workspace,command,network,data,action}`、`PolicyRule.severity in {block,warn,info}`、`Defect.source in {gate,verifier,user,supervisor}`、`Defect.state in {proposed,accepted,fixed,rejected}`，并要求 policy/rule id、expr、defect taskId/evidenceRef、proposal summary/expectedChange/targetRefs 非空；`isNonEmptyStringArray` 也会拒绝 `targetRefs: []` 这类空 ref 数组。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 都加入 malformed Policy / Defect snapshot matrix，覆盖 bad policy scope、bad policy severity、empty policy expr、bad defect source、bad defect state、empty defect evidenceRef、empty defect targetRefs 与 empty defect targetRef item；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official/prototype enum helper、非空数组 helper 与 gate fixtures。该证据只证明 projection ingress 会拒绝 malformed Policy / Defect envelopes，不证明完整 Policy engine、Defect governance lifecycle、Station projection production 或真实 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official / prototype budget projection parity 当前有 controlled/local evidence：projection contract 将 `workspace.budget` 登记为 optional read-only descriptor，并以 `budgetSurface.budgetStatuses` 机器契约持有 `ok / warning / danger / blocked` 状态 taxonomy；`budgetSurface.controlledEvidence` 进一步把该状态 taxonomy、official/prototype guard、`atelier:official-frontend-gate`、`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 与 `atelier:budget-surface-controlled-gate` evidence files 绑定为 `controlled_local_upstream` / `CONTROLLED_LOCAL_UPSTREAM`，并固定 `readOnlyProjection=true`、`appletBudgetWriteExposed=false`、`stationAggregationProven=true`、`providerBillingReconciliationProven=true`、`budgetCircuitBreakerProven=true`、`decisionCardRecoveryProven=true` 与 `realHostStationAppletE2EProven=false`。codegen 生成 official/browser prototype 共用的 `AtelierBudgetStatus` 与 `ATELIER_BUDGET_STATUSES`，两侧 guard 不再维护本地 status allowlist。official frontend 与 browser prototype 都会校验 `budget.status`、summary 非空、dimensions 非空、dimension id/label/unit 非空、`used/cap/percent` 非负且 `percent <= 100`，并拒绝 malformed status、空 summary、空 dimensions、负数 used、超过 100% 与未知 dimension status。official `BudgetBar` 与 browser prototype `BudgetStrip` 只展示 Station projection 的 money/token/time/cap 维度、status、summary 与 decision hint；legacy `budgetSpent/budgetCap` 仍是 fallback；UI 文案明确预算熔断、提额与恢复必须走 Station decision route，不暴露 budget halt/resume execution method。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 固定 valid/malformed budget snapshot matrix，`atelier:budget-surface-controlled-gate` 复跑 Station DirectRun budget usage ledger、BudgetUsageReconciler、pre-provider budget circuit breaker service tests，并新增 budget DecisionCard recovery service-level route proof：`TestResolveCollaborationInterruptTxRoutesHumanDecisionBudgetContinue` 证明 budget interrupt 的 human decision choice 只通过 Station `atelier.escalation.resolve` 标准化为 `human_decision_reason=budget` / `human_decision_action=continue` / `human_decision_transition=escalated->executing`，恢复由 Station orchestration 持有，payload 不包含 `budget.write`、`budget.resume`、`provider.invoke`、`runtime.execute`、`shell.execute` 或 `input_snapshot`。`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 optional contract field、generated budget taxonomy、controlledEvidence metadata、official/prototype guard、UI disclosure、Station budget recovery test anchor 与 forbidden `budget.halt` / `budget.resume`。该证据证明 budget descriptor 的 projection ingress、本地只读 UI parity、Station budget usage aggregation、provider billing reconciliation、Budget circuit breaker 与 budget DecisionCard recovery service-level 行为；不证明真实 provider implementations 填充外部 billing、真实 Desktop Host + applet DecisionCard recovery、真实 provider/live stream 或真实 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official / browser central stream overflow 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 的中央 projection stream 最多展开最新 8 个 block，并显示被折叠的较早 stream block 数量；Decision block 最多展开 3 个 option，但会优先保留 recommended / chosen option 并显示剩余 option 数量；official 与 browser prototype 的 Nego voices 都最多展开 4 条，并显示剩余 Station negotiation voice 数量，避免 compact projection row 静默吞掉 Station voices。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 `visibleSelectedBlocks`、`visibleDecisionOptions`、`hiddenDecisionOptionCount`、`hiddenVoiceCount` 与 `atelier.stream.moreProjected / atelier.decision.moreOptions / atelier.nego.moreVoices` 文案，并截取 official decision option source，要求点击只 `onResolveDecision(taskId, block.id, option.text)`，拒绝 resume/rerun/execute/provider/gate rerun/diff apply capability；`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 同步截取 browser prototype `DecisionCard` 与 `choose` source，要求 prototype 只调用 `runtime.resolveDecision({ taskId, blockId, choice })`，browser decision async success 和 failure 都由 task + block + choice request key 拥有，late success / late failure 会被 stale guard 拒绝，当前 failure 只复用 bridge recovery status；并固定 browser `NegoRow` 使用 `visibleVoices = b.voices.slice(0, 4)` 与 hidden Station voice disclosure。该证据只证明 official/browser UI 不静默隐藏中央 projection 内容，且 DecisionCard 只提交 human choice resolve intent；不证明真实 orchestration resume、协商运行时或人工裁决 E2E。

Official DiffCard projection parity 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official projection contract 新增 `streamBlocks.allowedKinds` 与 `diffSummaryFields`，official frontend 与 browser prototype 都只接受 contract-listed stream block kind；`kind=diff` 时必须具备 generated `diffSummaryFields`、非负 `files/added/removed` 与非空 `paths[]`，否则 projection event fail-closed。official `DiffSummary` 只展示 `N files changed +X -Y` 与可展开 path refs，折叠 path 时显示剩余 Station diff path ref 数量，不渲染 raw diff/patch/body，也不触发 apply/run。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 固定 valid/malformed/empty-path diff stream fixtures，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official/prototype generated contract constants、diff-specific guard 与 card wiring。该证据只证明 official central stream 的 diff metadata card 与 browser prototype `DiffCard` / projection guard 达成本地 parity，不证明真实 CodingProvider 产 `diff.patch`、artifact body fetch、Host sandbox renderer、真实 provider execution 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official / prototype stream block required-field guard 当前有 controlled/local evidence：projection contract 新增 `streamBlocks.requiredFieldsByKind`，并由 codegen 导出 `ATELIER_STREAM_BLOCK_REQUIRED_FIELDS_BY_KIND`；official frontend 与 browser prototype ingress guard 会在渲染前按 block kind 要求 `user.text`、`agent.text`、nego summary/voices/consensus、decision question/options/rollbackImpact/spentSoFar、artifact name/fileKind/producedBy 与 diff summary fields 存在。`streamBlocks.controlledEvidence` 进一步固定 `readiness=controlled_local_upstream`、`evidenceClass=CONTROLLED_LOCAL_UPSTREAM`、`atelier:projection-contract-gate` / `atelier:official-frontend-gate` / `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate`、allowed kinds、required fields by kind、`diffSummaryFields`、`officialProjectionIngressGuardProven=true`、`prototypeProjectionIngressGuardProven=true`、`diffMetadataOnlyCardProven=true`、`rawDiffPatchRenderingForbidden=true`、`appletStreamProducerExposed=false`、`realStreamProducerRuntimeProven=false`、`realProviderExecutionProven=false`、`realArtifactBodyRendererProven=false`、`realDesktopProductWindowUIProven=false` 与 `realHostStationAppletE2EProven=false`。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 固定 missing user/decision/artifact required-field fixtures，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 contract/generator/generated constants、controlledEvidence 与 official/prototype helper wiring。该证据只证明 malformed stream metadata fail-closed 与 diff metadata-only card，不证明真实 stream producer、provider execution、artifact body/render、真实 Desktop product window UI 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official / prototype known-task projection guard 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 与 browser prototype snapshot ingress 会先从 `workspace.tasks[]` 构建 known task id set，`selectedTaskId`、`streams`、`todos`、`contexts`、`artifacts`、`gates` 与 `replay` 的 task-scoped keys 必须绑定到已投影 task；runtime patch apply 也只允许 `task.upsert` 创建/更新 task，其余 `task.status`、`stream.append`、`decision.resolved`、`artifact.upsert`、`gate.upsert`、`context.replace`、`todo.replace` 必须引用已存在 task，否则 fail-closed，不创建 orphan bucket，也不推进 event memory / seq。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 固定 unknown task bucket / unknown task event fixtures，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official/prototype helper wiring 与 gate fixtures。该证据只证明 applet/prototype 不消费 orphan task-scoped projection metadata，不证明 Station projection producer、task lifecycle runtime 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-03 UI status 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official frontend 的 `deriveAtelierViewStatus` 优先级固定为 loading → auth-denied/disconnected typed errors → generic error → reconciling/degraded → empty → ready；`deriveAtelierViewStatus` 的 typed recovery type guard 返回 generated `AtelierTypedRecoveryKind`，不再用本地 `Extract<AtelierViewStatus, 'auth-denied' | 'disconnected'>` 硬编码 typed recovery subset；`useAtelierController` 现在保留 event-stream subscribe failure 的 `eventStreamErrorKind`，因此已有 snapshot 时 auth-denied / disconnected 不会被 degraded 掩盖，普通 stream failure 仍保持 degraded projection；subscription effect 启动或按 task/replay cursor reconnect 时会先进入 `subscribing` transition，因此已有 snapshot surface 显示 reconciling 而不是继续伪装为 live/ready；`AtelierAppletPage` 的页面组合逻辑已抽为 `application/pageComposition.ts` 的 `deriveAtelierPageSurface` pure view-model，统一输出 `globalErrorVisible`、`typedRecoveryKind`、`statusNotice`、`loadingVisible`、`emptyVisible`、`mainContentVisible`。`statusNotice` subset 由 `viewSurface.statusNoticeKinds` 生成的 `ATELIER_STATUS_NOTICE_KINDS` 驱动，不再在 official page composition 本地硬编码 reconciling/degraded；empty CTA 只在 `viewStatus=empty` 时出现，空 workspace 下的 auth-denied / disconnected 会展示 typed recovery panel，reconciling / degraded 不会被 empty CTA 覆盖；已有 snapshot 下的 auth-denied / disconnected typed recovery 与 reconciling/degraded notice 会保留 main content。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 已补 empty workspace 与 event-stream degraded 下 auth-denied、disconnected、reconciling、degraded、partial replay 的状态派生矩阵、stream connecting transition → reconciling 矩阵、page composition empty / typed recovery 矩阵，以及 full page-surface matrix，并用 `generic error owns page surface`、`auth-denied snapshot preserves main content with typed recovery`、`degraded snapshot preserves main content with notice` 和 disconnected/reconciling cases 固定 global error / typed recovery / notice 不覆盖或泄漏已有 projection 内容；`official page surface matrix must cover every generated view status`、`official page surface status notice matrix must cover every generated status notice kind`、`ATELIER_VIEW_STATUSES` / `ATELIER_TYPED_RECOVERY_KINDS` 集合等价断言证明矩阵覆盖每个 generated status / typed recovery / status notice kind；同时校验 zh-CN 状态 chip 不回退英文。browser prototype 侧已把 `RecoveryPanel` 的视觉语义抽为 `derivePrototypeRecoveryView` pure helper，并把最终页面显示条件收敛到 `derivePrototypePageSurface`：loading 独占页面，empty 只在 ready/无 stream 时显示，disconnected/auth-denied/reconciling/degraded/error 等 non-loading recovery 状态会显示 RecoveryPanel 且在已有 stream 时保留 stream projection。browser prototype 现在还提供 `?atelierStatus=loading|empty|ready|reconciling|degraded|disconnected|auth-denied|error` controlled visual scenario，场景集合直接派生自 generated `ATELIER_VIEW_STATUSES`，用同一 `derivePrototypePageSurface` 渲染每个 generated status，便于人工视觉检查 loading/empty/ready/reconciling/degraded/disconnected/auth-denied/error；该入口会拒绝 execution-shaped 或 runtime-capability-shaped status，不调用 provider/run/shell。`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 的 `testPrototypeRecoveryViewMatrix` 固定 loading/empty/auth-denied/disconnected/degraded/reconciling/error/ready 的 severity、symbol 与 retry button visibility，并用 `ATELIER_VIEW_STATUSES` 证明 recovery matrix 和 controlled visual scenario matrix 均覆盖每个 generated status；`prototype recovery severity matrix must match generated severity taxonomy` 与 `prototype recovery symbol matrix must match generated symbol taxonomy` 进一步证明 browser prototype recovery visual matrix 精确匹配 generated severity/symbol taxonomy。同 gate 的 prototype page-surface matrix 用 `prototype page surface matrix must cover every generated view status` 固定每个 generated status 均有最终页面显示条件证据，其中 `empty status owns recovery panel without duplicate empty affordance` 防止 explicit empty status 与 ready/no-stream empty affordance 重复显示，`error preserves stream with recovery panel` 固定 error recovery 保留已有 stream projection，loading 独占页面，auth-denied/disconnected/reconciling/degraded 保留或阻断 stream 行为按矩阵执行；其中 auth-denied 与 contract/official 一致不显示 retry；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 helper wiring 与 exhaustive matrix anchors。该证据只证明 official/frontend 和 browser prototype 的本地状态派生、文案与 recovery panel 语义，不证明真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet 断线/鉴权 E2E。

Official / prototype recovery retry affordance 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`atelier-projection.contract.json` 的 `viewSurface` 已成为 `loading / empty / ready / reconciling / degraded / disconnected / auth-denied / error`、event stream states、typed recovery kinds、empty CTA、recovery kinds、recovery tone/retry 与 prototype severity/symbol 的 machine-readable taxonomy；`atelier:projection-codegen` 将该 taxonomy 生成到 official frontend 与 browser prototype。official `AtelierErrorKind` 现在只是 generated `AtelierRecoveryKind` alias，不再维护本地 `'auth-denied' | 'disconnected' | 'invalid-projection' | 'agent-ids-required' | 'error'` union；generator 校验 `viewSurface.recovery.kinds` 与 `toneByKind` keys 对齐，official recovery label/tone map 用 `satisfies Record<AtelierErrorKind,...>` 做 exhaustive check。official `deriveAtelierViewStatus` / `atelierTypedRecoveryKind` / `deriveOfficialRecoveryView` / `nextAtelierEventStreamRetryDelayMs` 与 prototype `derivePrototypeRecoveryView` 不再各自维护第二套状态矩阵，而是消费 generated constants；official `ErrorPanel` 与 event-stream retry 都按 `ATELIER_RECOVERY_RETRYABLE_KINDS` 判定 Retry，prototype `statusFromBridgeError` 对 auth-denied 也返回 `retryable=false`，`auth-denied`、`invalid-projection`、`agent-ids-required` 不显示 Retry 且不会触发 event-stream retry，`disconnected` 与 generic `error` 才显示/触发 Retry，避免把不可由 Applet 自行恢复的鉴权/投影契约错误误导成可重试操作；`official recovery retry visibility matrix must cover every generated retryable recovery kind` 进一步证明 official recovery panel 的 Retry 可见性与 generated retryable taxonomy 集合等价，`prototype recovery retry visibility matrix must cover every generated retryable kind` 同步证明 browser prototype recovery panel 只对 generated retryable kinds 暴露 Retry。official typed recovery call-site 现在只传 `pageSurface.typedRecoveryKind` 与 `controller.eventStreamError`，不再在 page source 中硬编码 `auth-denied ? authDenied : disconnected` label fallback；`ErrorPanel` 统一用 `deriveOfficialRecoveryView(kind)` 的 `titleKey/detailKey/retryVisible/tone` 渲染，并用 `rawMessageVisible` 避免空 typed recovery message 被二次展示。visual tone 由 generated `ATELIER_RECOVERY_TONE_BY_KIND` 精确驱动，official helper 不允许 `?? 'danger'` fallback 或 `Record<string, AtelierRecoveryTone>` 宽化，global fallback 也必须读取 generated `error` tone。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 view/recovery/event-stream retry matrix、typed recovery call-site label-agnostic、ErrorPanel helper-owned detail fallback 与 no silent danger fallback，`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 固定 prototype auth-denied non-retry matrix，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 contract、generator、generated contract、page/helper/gate wiring。该证据只证明 projection surface status taxonomy 与 official/prototype recovery affordance 的 contract-backed controlled parity，不证明真实鉴权恢复、网络恢复、真实 Desktop product window UI 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official view-state unit evidence 还固定 loading 作为 top-level UI state：`atelierViewState.test.ts` 证明 loading 会压过 typed recovery、event-stream degraded、replay backlog 与 empty CTA，且 page surface 在 loading 时不显示 typed recovery、status notice、empty CTA 或 main content；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定该 unit test anchor。该证据只证明 official frontend controlled/local view-state precedence，不证明真实 product-window loading、真实空 workspace product window、真实网络故障或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official view-state unit evidence 同步固定 empty CTA 的最低优先级：`atelierViewState.test.ts` 证明 taskCount=0 时，typed recovery、reconciling、event-stream degraded 与 replay backlog 仍会先于 empty；page surface 在 disconnected / degraded empty workspace 中不会显示 empty CTA，而是分别显示 typed recovery 或 status notice；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定这些 unit test anchors。该证据只证明 official frontend controlled/local empty precedence，不证明真实空 workspace product window、真实 Host failure producer、真实 Station stream failure 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official disconnected empty workspace evidence 同步固定断连空态的可重试 recovery：`atelierViewState.test.ts` 证明 disconnected + taskCount=0 时 page surface 仍显示 typed recovery、隐藏 empty CTA 与 main content，并通过 generated recovery view 保持 `retryVisible=true` / warning tone；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定该 unit test anchor。该证据只证明 official frontend controlled/local disconnected empty-state precedence and retry affordance，不证明真实 Desktop Host disconnect producer、真实 Station network failure E2E、真实 product-window disconnected 视觉状态或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-05 stale snapshot failure vs fresher event 也有 controlled/local evidence：snapshot Host calls 现在通过 `applyErrorStatus: false` 延后 error status 写入，只有 `callSnapshot()` 在完成 `snapshotCallToken + projectionRevision` stale 判断后才允许当前 snapshot failure 写 recovery status；因此 in-flight `workspace.load` 的旧 failure 不会覆盖更晚 projection stream event 已恢复的 ready surface，也不会删除更晚 event append 的 stream block。`testBridgeSnapshotStaleFailureDoesNotClearFresherEvent` 与 `block-survives-stale-failure` 固定该路径，和既有 stale snapshot success vs fresher event 测试一起证明 success/failure 两类旧 snapshot response 都不能覆盖 fresher projection event。该证据只证明 browser bridge runtime controlled/local freshness guard，不证明真实 Desktop Host event stream producer、Station SSE 网络故障矩阵、跨重启 cursor recovery、真实 product-window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-05 current snapshot failure typed recovery 也有 controlled/local evidence：`applyErrorStatus: false` 只禁止底层 `call()` 在 stale 判断前提前写 error status，不会吞掉当前 snapshot Host call 的真实失败；`callSnapshot()` 在确认 snapshot response 仍是当前 projection owner 后会写入 `statusFromBridgeError(error)`。`testBridgeSnapshotCurrentFailureStillWritesTypedRecovery` 证明当前 `workspace.load` 的 `PERMISSION_DENIED current workspace load` 会稳定进入 `auth-denied` recovery，结构化 `CONNECTION_CLOSED` 会进入 retryable `disconnected` recovery，`CAPABILITY_FAILED current workspace load` 会进入 retryable generic `error` recovery，并保留当前 projection state。该证据只证明 browser bridge runtime controlled/local fail-closed recovery path，不证明真实 Desktop Host permission producer、真实 Station auth/network failure E2E 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Artifact preview sandboxRef canonical guard 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`artifactPreview.sandboxRefShape` 是 canonical `atelier-sandbox://<task>/<artifact>/preview` 的 contract source-of-truth，并生成 `ATELIER_ARTIFACT_SANDBOX_REF_SHAPE` 供 official client / official projection guard / browser applet-sdk bridge / browser prototype projection guard 消费；Desktop Host adapter 直接消费 contract JSON 的 `sandboxRefShape` / `bodyRefShape`。`artifactPreview.controlledEvidence` 进一步固定 `readiness=controlled_local_upstream`、`evidenceClass=CONTROLLED_LOCAL_UPSTREAM`、`atelier:projection-contract-gate` / `atelier:official-frontend-gate` / `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate`、allowed preview hints、metadata fields、previewTarget fields、canonical body/sandbox ref shape、forbidden raw body fields、`metadataOnlyProjectionProven=true`、`canonicalRefShapeGuardProven=true`、`hostPreviewIntentOnly=true`、`rawBodyProjectionForbidden=true`、`appletRendererExposed=false`、`realHostVisualRendererProven=false`、`realArtifactBodyFetchE2EProven=false`、`realDesktopProductWindowUIProven=false` 与 `realHostStationAppletE2EProven=false`。official/prototype projection guard、official client response guard、browser applet-sdk response guard、Desktop Gateway preview.open mapper 与 Desktop Host adapter 都要求 `atelier-sandbox://<task>/<artifact>/preview`，task/artifact segment 不允许空白；Desktop Host/Gateway 同时要求 canonical `artifact://<task>/<artifact>/body`。`atelier:official-frontend-gate`、`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate`、`atelier:projection-contract-gate`、Desktop applet vitest 与 targeted Rust test 固定 whitespace sandbox/body ref rejection。该证据只证明 metadata-only projection、projection/client/adapter/gateway 层 canonical ref guard 与 Host preview intent boundary，不证明真实 Host sandbox renderer、iframe/image/html rich preview、artifact body fetch runtime 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Artifact capability response API type 当前也有 controlled/local evidence：official client 与 browser prototype runtime 的 artifact body `bodyKind` 从 generated `ATELIER_ARTIFACT_BODY_KINDS` 派生，preview-open `mode / rendererOwner / rendererMode / rendererStatus` 从 generated preview-open taxonomy 派生；provider capability `scope / readOnly` 同样从 generated `ATELIER_PROVIDER_CAPABILITY_SCOPES` / `ATELIER_PROVIDER_CAPABILITY_READ_ONLY` 派生。该证据只证明调用侧 TypeScript API 边界不再宽化 generated descriptor，不证明真实 Station blob fetch、Host renderer 或 provider runtime。

DirectRun model selector taxonomy 当前有 controlled/static evidence：`atelier-projection.contract.json` 在 `methodPayloads['atelier.project.createFromGoal']` 下声明 `allowedDirectRunModels` 与 `defaultDirectRunModel`，并由 `atelier:projection-codegen` 生成 `ATELIER_DIRECT_RUN_MODELS`、`AtelierDirectRunModel` 与 `ATELIER_DEFAULT_DIRECT_RUN_MODEL`。official `RunTargetSelector` 与 browser prototype `RunPicker` 都从 generated `ATELIER_DIRECT_RUN_MODELS` 渲染 model tab，official controller 与 prototype mock / host empty state 都从 generated default 初始化；两端不再维护本地 `MODEL_OPTIONS` / `MODELS` 列表，browser prototype 也不再把 `trae-cli` / `claude-code` 展示为 DirectRun model 选项。Browser prototype 的 model selection async snapshot 由 selected task + selected model request key 拥有；late success 和 late failure 都会被 stale guard 拒绝，当前 model selection failure 只复用 bridge recovery status，不触发 provider/model/CLI 执行。该证据只证明 model selector taxonomy、默认值、request ownership 与 failure status recovery 的 contract-backed parity；选择 model 仍只提交 Station-owned `run.kind=model` / `run.model` intent，不调用 provider/model/CLI，不证明真实 DirectRun provider runtime、Desktop worker execution 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Agent Flow selector descriptor taxonomy 当前也有 controlled/static evidence：`atelier-projection.contract.json` 在 `methodPayloads['atelier.project.createFromGoal']` 下声明 `defaultAgentFlowId` 与 `agentFlowDescriptors`，并要求 descriptor id 与 `allowedAgentFlowIds` 对齐；`atelier:projection-codegen` 生成 `ATELIER_DEFAULT_AGENT_FLOW_ID`、`ATELIER_AGENT_FLOW_DESCRIPTORS` 与 `AtelierAgentFlowDescriptor`。official `RunTargetSelector` 与 browser prototype `RunPicker` 都从 generated descriptors 渲染 flow label/description/batch，official controller 与 prototype picker 都从 generated default 初始化；两端不再维护本地 `AGENT_FLOW_LABELS` / `AGENT_FLOW_DETAILS`。Station deterministic `EnginePolicy` schedule policy 已有 service/static evidence：六引擎 role order / parallel flag / convergenceMechanism 已由 Station pure function 定义，并被 collaboration node builder 消费，provider plan typed role 仍优先。该证据只证明 flow selector display/default taxonomy 与 Station schedule policy 的静态边界；选择 flow 仍只提交 Station-owned `run.kind=agents` / `run.flowId` intent，applet/prototype 不调度 agents、不执行 provider/model/CLI，不证明真实 provider 多引擎 runtime、Consensus/Objection/signoff 质量、真实 agents execution 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-05 subscription lifecycle 当前有 controlled/static evidence：projection event topic 与 Station stream subscription method 已由 contract generated `ATELIER_PROJECTION_EVENT_TOPIC` / `ATELIER_PROJECTION_SUBSCRIPTION_METHOD` 驱动，official client 与 browser applet bridge 不再本地硬编码 `atelier.projection.event` / `atelier.events.subscribe`；Desktop Gateway 已覆盖 Atelier projection subscription registry、duplicate reuse、`events.unsubscribe`/session destroy cancel、cursor persistence/restart recovery、bounded stream request timeout 与 reconnect backoff；browser bridge runtime gate 覆盖多 listener 复用单 subscription、last-listener cleanup、listener throw / snapshot 副本篡改隔离、applet bridge teardown 调 `events.unsubscribe`，并证明 successful subscribe setup 会短暂进入 reconciling，成功 `subscribeProjection` 拿到 cleanup 后会恢复 ready；订阅成功但暂无新 event 时不会一直停留在 reconciling，也不会伪装成 degraded/error。bridge runtime 同时证明 stale seq 只按 task/workspace scope 拒绝，`task-1` 的旧 event 不会污染 `task-2` 或 workspace scoped 后续 event。browser bridge runtime 的 event dedupe cache 由 `MAX_SEEN_EVENT_KEYS=500` 有界保存，`testBridgeDedupeCacheEvictsBoundedlyAndSeqGuardRejectsReplay` 证明超过上限后最旧 event id 会被 eviction，但同 id/旧 seq replay 仍会被 per-scope seq guard 拒绝，不会污染 projection stream；official reducer 侧也由 `MAX_ATELIER_EVENT_KEYS=500` 有界保存，`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定超过上限后最旧 id 被 eviction，且 evicted stale replay 仍进入 `stale` outcome、不会写入 stream。`appletBridge` 的 Station stream subscribe payload 现在会过滤空字段：只发送已定义 taskId，且只有 `afterEventSeq > 0` 才发送 replay cursor，避免把 `taskId: undefined` 或 `afterEventSeq: 0` 投给 Host/Station；`testAppletBridgeOmitsEmptyReplayCursorFields` 固定该行为。`testAppletBridgeExplicitProjectionStreamIntentWinsOverSnapshotCursor` 证明显式 `projectionStream.taskId/afterEventSeq` 优先于 initial snapshot replay cursor，避免 adapter 把用户指定的 Station stream intent 改写成 snapshot-derived cursor；`testAppletBridgeCorrelatesCustomProjectionTopicAcrossLifecycle` 证明 custom `projectionEventTopic` 会一致用于 `events.subscribe`、`host.onEvent` 与 `events.unsubscribe`，错误 topic event 不会进入 applet listener。browser bridge runtime 还会对 `subscribeProjection` 同步抛错、非函数 cleanup 返回值、cleanup 抛错 fail-closed：保留最后有效 snapshot，映射 `auth-denied` / `disconnected` 状态，清空本地 subscription slot，并允许后续 listener 重新建立订阅；`atelier-bridge-runtime-gate` 用 `testBridgeSubscriptionFailuresPreserveSnapshot`、`testBridgeMalformedUnsubscribeDoesNotStickSubscription`、`testBridgeCleanupFailureClearsSubscriptionAndPreservesSnapshot` 固定该行为。Host bridge event ingress 现在统一由 `createBridgeAtelierRuntime` 复用 `parseAtelierProjectionEvent`，`appletBridge` 只转发 Host payload，不再在 adapter 层静默 drop malformed event；malformed object / JSON string、空 top-level `taskId`、空 task-scoped patch `taskId`、空 `decision.resolved.blockId/choice`、malformed `context.replace` usedPct/files、缺失/空 `id` 或未知 `kind` 的 malformed stream block、空 `task/artifact/gate` upsert id、缺失/空 `id/text` 或未知 `status` 的 malformed todo item 都不会进入 dedupe、seq 或 patch apply，保留旧 projection 并允许后续合法 event 恢复 ready；`testBridgeRejectsMalformedProjectionEventsAndRecovers`、`testBridgeRejectsEmptyTaskScopeEventsAndRecovers`、`testBridgeRejectsEmptyDecisionResolutionAndRecovers`、`testBridgeRejectsMalformedTaskContextAndRecovers`、`testBridgeRejectsMalformedStreamBlocksAndRecovers`、`testBridgeRejectsEmptyUpsertEntityIdsAndRecovers`、`testBridgeRejectsMalformedTodoItemsAndRecovers` 与 `testAppletBridgeForwardsMalformedEventsToRuntimeGuard` 固定该行为。若 Host bridge event callback 内部应用坏 snapshot patch 抛错，browser bridge runtime 也会保留旧 projection、进入 error 状态且不让异常逃出 Host dispatch，后续合法 event 可恢复 ready；`testBridgeEventApplyFailurePreservesSnapshotAndRecovers` 固定该恢复路径。official frontend gate 通过 `nextAtelierEventStreamRetryDelayMs` retry matrix 覆盖 snapshot 后 subscribe failure 的 500/1500/5000ms bounded retry，并要求 helper 消费 `ATELIER_RECOVERY_RETRYABLE_KINDS`，因此 auth-denied / invalid-projection / agent-ids-required hard stop 和成功后恢复 `live` 都由 generated taxonomy 固定。P3-10 已新增 Atelier real-product workspace + projection replay gate：`applet:atelier-real-product-gate` 启动 sqlite-backed Station gate server，通过 Desktop Gateway `network.request` 从 `/v1/workspace` 到 `/applets/atelier/v1/workspace`，真实调用 Station-owned `AtelierProjectionService.LoadWorkspace`；同一 gate 通过 `atelier.events.subscribe` 到 `/sub-agent/agent/events/subscribe`，真实调用 Station-owned `EventStreamService.ReplayTaskEvents`，并经 `events.poll` 读到 `atelier.projection.event` / `stream.append` patch；同 gate 还用独立 session 传 `afterEventSeq=1`，证明 Desktop Gateway 会把 replay cursor 传到 Station event stream，且不会回放 seq<=1 的旧 event；当前 gate server 会先在 connected 后、任何 replay event 前受控关闭一次 Station SSE，Desktop Gateway 必须以 `afterEventSeq=0` 重连并收到 seq=1；随后 gate server 还会在 first replay 后受控关闭 Station SSE，Desktop Gateway 必须用 persisted cursor `afterEventSeq=1` reconnect 并收到 seq=2。证据写入 `tooling/acceptance/evidence/applets/official-applet/atelier-real-product-gate.json`，`evidenceClass=REAL_PRODUCT_PATH`。这仍不等于真实 Desktop product window UI，也不等于任意网络故障或跨重启 cursor recovery E2E。

P2-05 browser bridge initial snapshot ingress 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`initialSnapshot` 也统一经过 `assertAtelierProjectionSnapshot` 再转换为 runtime snapshot，`testBridgeRejectsMalformedInitialSnapshot` 证明 malformed initial projection 不会创建 runtime state。该证据只证明 browser prototype runtime 构造入口 fail-closed，不证明真实 Desktop Host initial payload、Station SSE 任意网络故障、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-05 browser bridge non-snapshot status race 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`createBridgeAtelierRuntime` 用 `latestCallStatusToken` 固定 latest Host capability call owns runtime status completion，并用 `activeCallRestorableStatus` 记住进入 loading 前的稳定状态；较早 non-snapshot call 的 stale success / stale failure 不能覆盖较晚 call 已经写入的 auth-denied / error / ready 状态。`testBridgeNonSnapshotStaleSuccessDoesNotClearNewerError` 证明 provider capabilities 的较早成功不会清掉 memory confirmation 的较晚 auth-denied；`testBridgeNonSnapshotStaleFailureDoesNotClearNewerSuccess` 证明 provider capabilities 的较早 stale failure 不会清掉 memory confirmation 的较晚 success / ready。当前 non-snapshot failure 也有 recovery matrix：`testBridgeNonSnapshotErrorsUpdateStatus` 证明 `FORBIDDEN provider discovery` 进入 non-retryable `auth-denied`，结构化 `CONNECTION_CLOSED` 的 `opaque provider discovery` 进入 retryable `disconnected`，`CAPABILITY_FAILED provider discovery` 进入 retryable generic `error`，且不改写当前 projection state。browser `appletBridge` 现在会在 snapshot assertion / typed response guard 前统一规范化 Host error envelope：resolved `{ error: { code, message } }`、rejected `{ code, message }` object、以及带 `code` 属性的 `Error` 都会抛出保留 code/message 与 machine-readable `Error.code` 的 `Atelier bridge method ... failed: CODE message`；browser `bridgeRuntime.ts` / `prototypeRecoveryView` 通过 generated `viewSurface.bridgeRuntimeRecoveryCodeKindByCode` 优先按结构化 code 分类，且直接识别 `{ error: { code } }` Host envelope，`PERMISSION_DENIED/FORBIDDEN/UNAUTHORIZED/AUTH_DENIED` 稳定进入 `auth-denied`，`NETWORK_DISCONNECTED/NETWORK_ERROR/TIMEOUT/STREAM_DISCONNECTED/CONNECTION_CLOSED` 稳定进入 `disconnected`，`CAPABILITY_FAILED/INVALID_PROJECTION/UNKNOWN` 仍保持 generic `error`，message substring 只作为 legacy fallback。因此 code-only message 也不会退成 invalid snapshot 或 `[object Object]` generic error；`testAppletBridgeNormalizesHostErrorEnvelopes` 固定 resolved/rejected/code-only/unknown structured code matrix，`prototypeRecoveryView.test` 固定 direct nested Host error envelope recovery matrix。该证据只证明 browser prototype runtime local status hygiene 与 Host error envelope fail-closed 归一化，不改变 Host capability response truth、Station provider/memory truth，也不证明真实 Desktop Host 并发 capability 调用、真实 product window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official frontend error normalization 当前也有 controlled/local evidence：`normalizeAtelierError()` 会先保留显式 `atelier.error.*` key，再通过 generated `viewSurface.bridgeRuntimeRecoveryCodeKindByCode` 对 Host/Service structured `code` 分类，最后才用 lowercase message 做 legacy fallback；direct `{ error: { code } }` envelope 也会在 fallback 前被识别。`PERMISSION_DENIED/FORBIDDEN/UNAUTHORIZED/AUTH_DENIED` 稳定映射到 `atelier.error.authDenied` / `auth-denied`，`NETWORK_DISCONNECTED/NETWORK_ERROR/TIMEOUT/STREAM_DISCONNECTED/CONNECTION_CLOSED` 稳定映射到 `atelier.error.disconnected` / `disconnected`，`CAPABILITY_FAILED/INVALID_PROJECTION/UNKNOWN` 保持 generic `error`。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 的 official error normalization matrix 覆盖 uppercase/mixed-case legacy message、structured `Error.code`、structured `cause.error.code`、plain structured error object、direct nested structured error envelope、explicit Atelier error key preservation；`atelier:official-frontend-unit-gate` 的 `atelierErrorClassification.test.ts` 同步覆盖 direct nested envelope，不依赖大 gate matrix 作为唯一证据；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 generated taxonomy consumption、legacy lowercase fallback、unit test anchors 与 executable matrix anchors。该证据只证明 official frontend 本地错误分类不会因 Host/Service structured code、message 大小写或 forbidden 变体退化成 generic error，不证明真实 Desktop Host error producer、真实 Station auth/network failure E2E 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-05 browser bridge snapshot call race 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`createBridgeAtelierRuntime` 用 `latestSnapshotCallToken` 固定 latest snapshot Host capability call owns projection writes，并用 `projectionRevision` 记录 stream event / snapshot response / local projection mutation 的投影 freshness；snapshot Host call 通过 `applyErrorStatus: false` 延后底层 error status 写入，`callSnapshot()` 在写入 projection snapshot 或 error status 前都会校验 `snapshotCallToken !== latestSnapshotCallToken` 与 `projectionRevisionAtCall !== projectionRevision`，旧 workspace.load success / failure 只返回当前 snapshot，不覆盖较新的 sendMessage / setStatus / purge 等 snapshot call 已经写入的 projection 或 ready 状态，也不会覆盖 in-flight 期间先到达的 fresher projection event 或 local model intent。`testBridgeSnapshotStaleSuccessDoesNotOverwriteNewerSnapshot` 证明旧 `workspace.load` success 不会把 `task-stale` 插入并覆盖较新的 snapshot；`testBridgeSnapshotStaleSuccessDoesNotOverwriteFresherEvent` 证明旧 `workspace.load` success 不会删除先到达的 `block-from-fresher-event` stream block；`testBridgeSnapshotStaleFailureDoesNotClearFresherEvent` 证明旧 `workspace.load` failure 不会删除先到达的 `block-survives-stale-failure` stream block 或清掉 ready status；`testBridgeSnapshotStaleSuccessAfterLocalModelIntentRestoresStatus` 与 `testBridgeSnapshotStaleFailureAfterLocalModelIntentRestoresStatus` 证明旧 `workspace.load` success / failure 不会覆盖本地 `setModel()` intent，并且不会把 runtime surface 卡在 loading；`testBridgeSnapshotStaleFailureDoesNotClearNewerSnapshot` 证明旧 `workspace.load` failure 不会把较新的 ready snapshot 打回 error/auth-denied。该证据只证明 browser prototype runtime local projection ownership，不改变 Host capability response truth、Station projection truth，也不证明真实 Desktop Host 并发 snapshot capability 调用、真实 product window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-08 official DirectRun model intent parity 当前补齐 controlled/local evidence：browser prototype 已通过 `buildPrototypeRunTarget()` 保持 model branch 只携带 `run.kind=model` / `run.model`，agents branch 才携带 generated `flowId`；official `buildAtelierProjectCreateIntent()` 现在同样使用 discriminated intent，`runKind === 'model'` 分支不会输出 `flowId`，且输入里的 flow id（包括无效 flow id）不会污染 DirectRun model intent 或 submit key。`projectCreateActionGuards.test.ts` 的 `omits flowId from DirectRun model project create intent` 与 `ignores flow ids for DirectRun model project create intent` 固定该 parity；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定源码与测试锚点。该证据只证明 official/prototype create-from-goal intent shape parity，不证明真实 DirectRun provider execution、真实 Station project creation E2E、real IDE launch、production provider/runtime quality 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-08 artifact preview target kind taxonomy 当前补齐 controlled/local evidence：`artifactPreview.allowedPreviewTargetKinds` 现在显式固定为 Host renderer kinds `markdown/web/image/diff`，并生成 `ATELIER_ARTIFACT_PREVIEW_TARGET_KINDS`；official 与 browser prototype projection ingress 都用该 generated taxonomy 校验 `previewTarget.kind`，不再把 broader artifact kind 或任意 string 当作 Host preview renderer kind。official `buildAtelierArtifactPreviewOpenIntent()` 与 prototype `buildPrototypeArtifactPreviewOpenIntent()` 也在 intent 进入 `atelier.artifact.preview.open` 前 reject 非 generated kind，`html` 现在在 unit 层 fail-closed。该证据只证明 applet projection/intent surface 的 preview target kind fail-closed，不证明真实 Desktop product-window webview renderer、真实 artifact blob fetch/render、Console Logs runtime stream、Host Storage runtime 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-05 event task-scope correlation 当前已补齐 official / browser prototype parity：official `parseAtelierProjectionEvent` 与 browser `parseAtelierProjectionEvent` 都要求 top-level `event.taskId` 与 patch task scope 一致；snapshot event 不允许携带 task scope，task.upsert 顶层 taskId 若存在必须匹配 upserted task id，其它 task-scoped patch 必须满足 `event.taskId === patch.taskId`。`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 用 `evt-snapshot-with-task-scope`、`evt-task-upsert-mismatched-task-scope`、`evt-mismatched-event-patch-task-scope`、`evt-mismatched-task-1-task-2-seq-1` 证明坏 event 在 parser 层变为 null，不会进入 reducer 以 task-1 seq scope 修改 task-2 projection；`atelier-bridge-runtime-gate` 用同名 snapshot/upsert fixtures 与 `testBridgeRejectsMismatchedEventPatchTaskScopeAndRecovers` 证明 browser bridge runtime 不会创建 task-3 或污染 task-2 stream，后续合法 task-2 event 可恢复 ready。该证据只证明 local parser/reducer/runtime fail-closed 行为，不证明真实 Desktop Host event producer、真实 Station SSE 任意网络故障、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-05 event-stream retry taxonomy 补充：official `nextAtelierEventStreamRetryDelayMs` 当前消费 generated `ATELIER_RECOVERY_RETRYABLE_KINDS`，不是维护独立 hard-coded non-retryable list；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 已固定 disconnected/error 500/1500/5000ms bounded retry、超过次数停止、无 snapshot 不 retry，以及 auth-denied / invalid-projection / agent-ids-required 不 retry；`event stream retry matrix must cover every generated retryable recovery kind` 和 `event stream retry matrix must hard-stop every generated non-retryable recovery kind` 进一步证明 retry matrix 与 generated retryable/non-retryable taxonomy 集合等价，不会在 contract 增减 recovery kind 时只保留旧样例。official `ErrorPanel` 的 Retry 也只绑定 `controller.load` projection reload，并显示 `atelier.recovery.retryBoundary` 文案，不会被误读为 execution / rerun / provider invoke。browser prototype `RecoveryPanel` 的 Retry 按钮只绑定 `runtime.loadWorkspace()` projection reload，并显式标注 `Projection reload only; no execution, rerun, or provider invoke.`；`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` / `atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定该 affordance 不漂移成 execute/rerun/provider invoke。malformed Host projection event 也由 gate 固定为保留已有 snapshot surface 并进入 degraded / invalid-projection，后续合法 event 可恢复。这只证明 official/frontend 与 browser prototype 本地 retry policy、malformed event recovery 与 projection contract taxonomy 对齐，不证明真实 Station SSE 任意网络故障、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official malformed / rejected projection event handling 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official `subscribeAtelierProjectionEvents` 不再静默丢弃 malformed Host projection event；parse 失败会通过 `stateFromMalformedAtelierProjectionEvent` 让已有 snapshot surface 进入 `degraded` / `invalid-projection`，后续合法 event 仍走普通 reducer 路径恢复 `live` 并清空 event stream error。official reducer 也会返回 `applied / duplicate / stale / unknown-task` outcome；controller 只把 applied/duplicate event 视为 live，不会在 stale seq 或 unknown-task scope rejected event 后误报 `live`，而是保留最后有效 projection 并进入 degraded / invalid-projection。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 client callback、controller wiring、pure helper 状态矩阵、reducer outcome matrix 与后续合法 event recovery patch；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 source wiring。该证据只证明 official frontend 本地 malformed/rejected event fail-closed 行为，不证明真实 Station SSE 任意网络故障、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Browser applet bridge subscription invoke/release cleanup 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`appletBridge` 通过 `invokeProjectionSubscription` 处理 `events.subscribe` / `atelier.events.subscribe` / `events.unsubscribe` 的 rejected promise，记录 warning 但不产生 unhandled rejection；若 Host 缺少 `host.onEvent`，adapter 会 fail-fast，`createBridgeAtelierRuntime` 保留最后有效 projection 并进入 `disconnected`，不会把缺失事件能力伪装成 ready。成功订阅 release 时，adapter 会先关闭本地 `host.onEvent` listener，再通过 `events.unsubscribe(topic=atelier.projection.event)` 释放 Host projection topic；`testAppletBridgeUnsubscribesProjectionTopicAndStream` 固定 release 后 late Host event 不会再进入 listener，且重复 release 不会重复 Host topic unsubscribe；`testAppletBridgeSuccessfulReleaseHandlesRejectedTopicUnsubscribe` 固定 successful release 期间 Host `events.unsubscribe` reject 只产生 bounded warning，不会形成 unhandled rejection，也不会重新打开 local listener 或投递 late event。若 `events.subscribe` 或 `atelier.events.subscribe` 被 Host reject，adapter 会 fail-closed：先关闭本地 `host.onEvent` listener，再通过 `events.unsubscribe(topic=atelier.projection.event)` 主动释放 Host projection topic，最后向 runtime 投递 `atelier.projection.subscription-rejected` typed recovery payload；若用户 release 先发生、async subscribe reject 后返回，adapter 只处理 rejected promise 和 warning，不向已释放 listener 投递 typed recovery，也不产生 unhandled rejection。runtime 通过 `projectionSubscriptionRejectedError` 保留原始 reason，并用既有 `statusFromBridgeError` 映射到 `auth-denied` / `disconnected` / `error`，而不是把订阅拒绝降级成 generic malformed projection event。`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 的 `testAppletBridgeHandlesRejectedSubscriptionInvokes` 固定 rejected `events.subscribe` 会 Host topic unsubscribe、release 后不重复 cleanup、且 `evt-after-rejected-subscription-invoke` 不会进入 applet listener；`testAppletBridgeCanonicalSubscriptionRejectionCleansLocalEventHandler` 固定 rejected `atelier.events.subscribe` 会 Host topic unsubscribe、release 后不重复 cleanup、且 `evt-after-canonical-subscription-rejection` 不会进入 runtime listener；`testAppletBridgeReleasedSubscriptionIgnoresLateRejectedInvokes` 固定 release-before-late-reject cleanup race，`testAppletBridgeMissingEventBridgeFailsClosed` 固定缺失 `host.onEvent` fail-closed，`testAppletBridgeRejectsEmptyProjectionStreamAgentIdFailClosed` 固定空 `agentId` invalid payload 不会调用 canonical Station subscription、会 Host topic unsubscribe、且 release after rejection 不会重复 cleanup，`testAppletBridgeSubscriptionRejectionMapsTypedRecovery` 固定 auth/network rejection 的 typed recovery 映射；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 helper、failure payload、typed recovery helper、release-before-late-reject、Host topic unsubscribe cleanup、successful release idempotence、successful release unsubscribe rejection handling 与 post-release/post-reject delivery 禁止项。该证据只证明 applet-sdk adapter 层对 successful release、successful-release cleanup rejection 与 rejected subscription invoke fail-closed 且无未处理 promise，不证明真实 Desktop Host + Station SSE 网络恢复、跨重启 cursor recovery 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Browser runtime bootstrap projection stream config 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`runtimeBootstrap` 通过 `normalizeProjectionStreamConfig` 统一规范 URL query 与 `window.__ATELIER_PROJECTION_STREAM__`，`agentId` 按 generated `eventSubscription.agentIdSourcePriority` 解析（显式 `agentId` 优先，global config 的 `agentIds[0]` fallback，URL query 的 repeated `agentIds` fallback），`taskId` 按 generated `eventSubscription.taskIdSourcePriority` 解析（certification-created selected task 条件命中时优先，其后 explicit `taskId`、controller selected task、snapshot selected task、snapshot first task；global config 与 URL query 均进入同一 resolver），空白 agent id 会被拒绝，空白 task id 会省略，`afterEventSeq` 只有 finite 且 `> 0` 才保留；低层 `prototypeProjectionSubscription` helper 现在也支持同一 generated taskId source priority 的 certification-created selected task、explicit task、controller selected task、snapshot selected task 与 snapshot first task resolver，并证明 `certificationMode/createGoal/selectedTaskId` 只参与本地 taskId 派生，不会进入最终 Host subscribe payload。`runtimeBootstrap` 自身保持 wiring-only：普通预览只 `return createMockAtelierRuntime()`，Host 模式只装配 `createBridgeAtelierRuntime()` + `createAppletSdkAtelierBridge(sdk, { projectionStream: readProjectionStreamConfig(initialSnapshot), initialSnapshot })`，不在启动阶段直接调用 workspace load、projection subscription、event topic subscription 或 provider/runtime/shell/file/memory/run/input_snapshot 等副作用。`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 的 `testRuntimeBootstrapNormalizesProjectionStreamConfig` 固定 Host runtime allowlist exact-match policy（只有精确 `lynx` / `web-host` 使用 applet bridge；普通 `browser` / `vite` / `node` / `desktop`、大小写变体 `Lynx`、空白包裹 ` lynx `、前后缀变体 `web-host-preview` 和空字符串都不使用 applet bridge）、Host seed 为空 projection surface 且不含 execution-shaped payload、空白 global config、trimmed URL/global config、zero/invalid replay cursor 处理，也固定 explicit global stream config 优先于 URL query、invalid global config 可回落 query params、query-only config 缺少 required `agentId` 时 fail-closed、显式 `agentId` 优先于 generated `agentIds[0]` fallback、空 `agentIds[0]` fail-closed、URL query `agentIds[0]` fallback、explicit `taskId` 优先于 selected/snapshot fallback，以及 global/query 两条入口的 controller selected、snapshot first、certification-created selected task fallback；`prototypeProjectionSubscription.test.ts` 的 `uses certification-created and controller selected task sources before snapshot fallback` 与 `builds payloads from certification-created and controller selected task source priority only` 固定低层 helper parity。`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 helper、测试名、wiring-only anchors、Host boundary anchors、exact allowlist anchors、generated agent/task id source priority anchors 与 source-policy anchors。该证据只证明 browser prototype bootstrap 与 bridge subscribe payload helper 不会在普通浏览器预览里误连 applet bridge，也不会在启动阶段绕过 bridge runtime 直接调用 Host capability，或把 mock tasks、execution payload、空白 agent id、无效 cursor、缺少 agent id 的 query-only config、certification/controller 上下文字段传给 applet bridge payload；不证明真实 Desktop Host launch config、Station SSE 恢复或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-05 bridge runtime projection subscribe refresh 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`createBridgeAtelierRuntime()` 在接受 fresh snapshot response 后会调用可选 `bridge.refreshProjectionSubscription?.(projection)`，让 applet bridge 用最新 projection snapshot 重新计算 generated subscription payload；`createAppletSdkAtelierBridge()` 保留 active projection listener / fail-closed close handler，并用 `lastProjectionStreamPayloadKey` 对刷新 payload 去重，只有 payload 变化时才重发 canonical `ATELIER_PROJECTION_SUBSCRIPTION_METHOD`。`testBridgeRuntimeRefreshesProjectionSubscribeAfterCreatedSelectedTask` 证明初始空 Host snapshot 的 product-window certification stream 先只发送 `{agentId, afterEventSeq:0}`，`runtime.createProjectFromGoal()` 返回 selected task 后会刷新为 `{agentId, taskId:'created-task', afterEventSeq:0}`，且最终 Host subscribe payload 不泄露 `certificationMode/createGoal/selectedTaskId` 或 provider/gate/artifact/trace/checkpoint/resume/memory/input_snapshot/shell/file/run 等 execution-shaped 字段。该证据只证明 browser bridge runtime/app bridge controlled refresh lifecycle，不证明真实 Desktop Host launch config、真实 Station SSE 任意失败矩阵、跨重启 cursor recovery、真实 product-window UI、real IDE launch、production provider/runtime quality 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

P2-03 official post-snapshot reconnect handoff 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official `useAtelierController` 在 `createProject`、`resolveDecision`、`sendMessage`、`setTaskLifecycle/purge` 等 snapshot-changing intent 成功返回新 projection 后，不再直接写入 `eventStreamState: 'live'`，而是复用 `stateFromAtelierEventStreamConnecting()` 进入 `subscribing`，由 `deriveAtelierViewStatus()` 派生为 `reconciling` 并保留已加载 projection surface；只有后续 `subscribeAtelierProjectionEvents()` 成功拿到 release 后才会把 stream state 更新为 `live`。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 对四个 snapshot success block 做切片断言，证明它们都先进入 reconnecting state，且 block 内不再直接声明 live；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定该 gate anchor。该证据只证明 official frontend 本地 post-snapshot UI handoff 不会在重订阅成功前误报 live/ready，不证明真实 Desktop Host launch config、真实 Station SSE 任意失败矩阵、跨重启 cursor recovery、真实 product-window UI、real IDE launch、production provider/runtime quality 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

## 对应设计

Browser prototype 从 [functional-modules.md](../execution-plans/functional-modules.md) §1「产品形态」推导，做到「对话流 + 协商内联可展开 + 内联决策」。当前 browser prototype 已和 official Lynx applet 的 controlled single-column projection shell 对齐；left rail / right preview panel 不再作为默认产品形态：

| 原型区域 | 对应设计 | 落地模块 |
|---------|---------|---------|
| Header / action rail：任务标题 + project/status metadata + Work/Code/Design intent preset + New task + Open in IDE Host intent + 预算条（成本熔断可见） | §1.2 / §1.5 | M7 / M13 |
| Task organizer section：管理方式 plugin 切换器 + 项目文件夹分组任务列表（进行中带 spinner / 分支图标）+ lifecycle sections | §1.2 | M11 / M12 |
| 对话流：用户气泡（含图片附件 chip）+ Atelier 复述目标卡（轻 markdown + 含 L0/L1/L2 验收口径）+ Completed + 反馈条 | §1.3 ① | M5 / M6 / M13 |
| 对话流内联「协商行」：折叠一行 / 展开看角色·带证据反对·折中·共识；无证据反对降级为疑虑 | §1.4 / §2 | M1 / M2 / M3 |
| 对话流内联「决策卡」：升级给人，列选项 + 推荐 + 已花成本 + 回滚影响，点选即推进 | §1.5 | M7 |
| 对话流「diff 卡 / 产物卡」：N files changed +X -Y 可展开文件列表 / 文件名回链到哪步哪个 Agent | §1.4 | M8 / M9 |
| Artifacts 托盘 + 右侧产物预览面板：metadata-only artifact preview + Host safe text fetch / Host sandbox preview intent；web iframe / image / html / diff rich renderer、真实 Console Logs runtime stream 与 attachment Host Storage 仍待 Host-owned runtime/E2E | §1.4 / §1.3 ④ | M14 / M8 |
| 右栏：TaskGraph projection first + legacy Todo fallback + Context（token 用量条 + Files/Other 触达文件），与产物预览面板三元切换，仅复杂任务出现 | §1.3 | M5 / M8 |
| 底部富输入框：Skills capability 插入斜杠命令 / 图片 / 模型选择器 / 发送 | §1.1 | M13 |

数据默认由 `src/mock.ts` seed 驱动，但 UI 已通过 `src/runtime.ts` 的 `AtelierRuntime` 访问 projection snapshot、provider capability discovery、feedback intent submit 和 workspace open intent。当前还不接真实后端，下一步联调只替换 runtime 实现。

源码结构（React + LobeUI/antd DOM 组件）：`src/types.ts`（对话流 block 模型 + metadata-only `Artifact`/`ConsoleLog` 产物模型 + body/previewTarget metadata 字段 + `TaskHost`/`TaskPlugin` 接口 + 只读 Project/TaskGraph projection 类型）、`src/runtime.ts`（Atelier projection runtime 契约 + mock-backed 默认实现 + runtime status + `fetchArtifactBody` safe text mock + `openArtifactPreview` Host sandbox renderer session mock）、`src/projection.ts`（projection snapshot / patch / method contract + runtime guard，并保留 `workspace.projects` roundtrip）、`src/bridgeRuntime.ts`（bridge-backed runtime + Host event parse guard + patch replay + seq guard + bridge error status）、`src/appletBridge.ts`（applet-sdk / Web Host 适配器 + snapshot response guard + Host event payload forwarding）、`src/theme.ts`（调色板 `C` + 角色色 `ROLE_COLOR` + 立场色 `STANCE`）、`src/mock.ts`（多项目任务含生命周期 + 只读 project/taskGraph seed + 各任务对话流：协商/决策/diff/产物/图片附件 + 各任务 metadata-only artifacts：markdown/web/image/diff）、`src/blocks.tsx`（各类 block 渲染：用户气泡含图片 chip / Atelier 含反馈条 / 协商行 / 决策卡 / 产物卡 / diff 卡 + 轻 markdown 内联）、`src/preview.tsx`（Artifacts 托盘 + 产物预览面板：Host safe text capability 区块 + Host sandbox preview capability 区块 + renderer session status + metadata-only artifact preview + projected paths / mock logs disclosure）、`src/plugins.tsx`（管理 plugin：默认 folders 项目分组 + 简单平铺 + 看板/DAG 占位）、`src/Page.tsx`（壳：Work/Code/Design toggle + 左栏导航含折叠/筛选 + plugin 加载 + 顶栏含 Open in 与工具图标 + runtime recovery layer + 对话流 + Artifacts 托盘 + 富输入框 + 右栏 TaskGraph projection first / legacy Todo fallback / Context 与产物预览三元切换 + safe body fetch / sandbox preview renderer session intent wiring）、`src/main.tsx`（Vite 入口）。构建配置：`vite.config.ts` / `index.html` / `tsconfig.json`。

## 已知差异 / 待补

- 管理 plugin 默认「项目文件夹分组」+ 可选「简单平铺」可用；看板 / DAG 为切换器占位，未实现 `render`。organizer mode / ready descriptor 已由 `workbenchSurface.taskOrganizerModes` 生成 `ATELIER_TASK_ORGANIZER_MODES`，默认 organizer mode 也由 `workbenchSurface.defaultTaskOrganizerMode` 生成 `ATELIER_DEFAULT_TASK_ORGANIZER_MODE`，official selector 与 browser prototype plugin registry 都消费该 generated descriptor/default，不再分别维护本地 `folders/flat-list/kanban/dag` union/order/ready list，也不再用 `ATELIER_TASK_ORGANIZER_MODES[0]` 或 `PLUGINS[0]` 从 descriptor/registry 顺序推导默认 plugin；browser prototype 的 unknown plugin id fallback 通过 `resolveTaskPlugin()` 回到 generated default。official left rail 已同步为 local read-only task organizer selector：`folders` / `flat-list` 只消费 Station task projection 并改变本地分组，`kanban` / `dag` 只显示 disabled boundary，UI 文案明确不重排、不调度、不执行、也不 replan 任务；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 `TaskOrganizerPanel`、folders/flat renderers、disabled placeholder、locale boundary copy、generated organizer mode descriptor consumption，并拒绝 `organizer.reorder` / `organizer.schedule` / `organizer.execute` / `organizer.replan` capability，同时禁止 disabled kanban/dag mode 下回退渲染 folders list。browser prototype 的 `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 也固定 `kanban` / `dag` plugin 仍为 generated `ready=false` / `未实现` placeholder、默认 plugin 来自 generated default，并拒绝 `organizer.reorder` / `organizer.schedule` / `organizer.execute` / `organizer.replan` 出现在 prototype plugin source；同一 gate 还固定 browser `TaskRow` lifecycle menu 只能调用 `host.setStatus(active/archived/deleted)`、`host.requestPurge()` 与 `host.purge()`，且保留 Station deleted-state precondition 文案，并拒绝 task execution / schedule / replan / purge bypass capability。official selector 已迁移 browser prototype folders/flat-list 内的 active / archived / deleted 分区：active 进入主列表，archived / deleted 进入默认折叠 lifecycle section；row action 复用现有 `setTaskLifecycle`，restore/archive/delete/purge 都只提交 Station-owned lifecycle intent，deleted task purge 仍走二次确认并显示 Station deleted-state precondition。browser prototype 的 task purge 与 official applet 对齐为二次确认：deleted task 首击只显示 `确认彻底删除` 和 Station deleted-state precondition 提示，第二击才提交 purge intent；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 `purgeConfirmId` / `requestPurge` / `确认彻底删除` / deleted-state 提示。该证据只证明 prototype / official organizer lifecycle selector affordance parity，不证明真实 Station purge E2E、看板/DAG runtime、任务重排/调度执行能力或 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
- Work/Code/Design 模式 toggle 与 header/action rail 已做**视觉态**（Work/Code/Design 只作为声明式 Station preset / `intentPreset`，official applet 也显示不会切换真实 IDE mode 或 workspace runtime 的边界；`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 固定 official `IntentPresetSelector` 只调用 `onPresetChange(option.id)` 写声明式 preset，并拒绝 `atelier.ide.mode`、`ide.mode.switch`、`workspace.mode.switch`、`runtime.mode.switch`、`provider.runtime.override` 或 `provider.invoke` 出现在 selector source；browser prototype run target selector 也只写入 Station-owned run intent，不在 applet 侧 invoke provider、run model 或 execute CLI；Terminal / Outline 在 browser prototype 已收敛为单列 content rail 内的 prototype-only inline disclosure，official applet 也显示 disabled boundary，明确未接 shell/execute capability 或真实 TaskGraph panel）。
  - Terminal / Outline placeholder 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official `TopbarToolBoundary` / `ToolBoundaryPill` 保持非交互，`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 会拒绝 `bindtap` / `onTap` / `onClick` / `onPress` handler 进入该边界；browser prototype 的 Terminal / Outline inline disclosure 固定为 `Prototype-only inline disclosure`，`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 会拒绝 `terminal.open` / `atelier.terminal.open` / `shell.execute` / `execute.shell` / `outline.open` / `atelier.outline.open` 能力名出现在 prototype page。该证据只证明 placeholder boundary 与 forbidden capability exposure，不证明 Terminal shell、真实 TaskGraph outline panel 或 Host+Station+applet E2E。
- Open in IDE 已同步到 browser prototype 与 official applet：task projection 带 `workspaceOpenTarget`，点击只提交 `atelier.workspace.open` / `workspace.open`，Desktop Host 只接受 `pt-workspace://task/<task_id>?workspace=<workspace_id>` schema intent；workspace open contract metadata 当前有 `methodPayloads['atelier.workspace.open'].controlledEvidence`，固定 `readiness=controlled_local_upstream`、`evidenceClass=CONTROLLED_LOCAL_UPSTREAM`、`method=atelier.workspace.open`、`intentOwner=desktop_host`、`sideEffectClass=host_ui`、`transportKind=desktop_gateway_host_local`、`frontendCall=sdk.invoke('atelier.workspace.open')`、required/optional/response fields、generated URI shape、forbidden actions、`hostIntentOnlyProven=true`、`officialResponseGuardProven=true`、`prototypeBridgeResponseGuardProven=true`、`requestCorrelationProven=true`、`generatedUriShapeGuardProven=true`、`desktopGatewayUriShapeProven=true`、`desktopGatewayRejectsNonContractUriProven=true`、`desktopGatewayNoNativeLaunchProven=true`、`appletFileShellExecuteExposed=false`、`appletOpenExternalUrlExposed=false`、`realIdeLaunchProven=false`、`realWorkspaceResolverProven=false`、`realSandboxRuntimeProven=false` 与 `realHostStationAppletE2EProven=false`。该 URI shape 由 `methodPayloads['atelier.workspace.open'].uriShape` 生成 `ATELIER_WORKSPACE_OPEN_URI_SHAPE`，official client、official projection guard、browser applet-sdk bridge 与 browser projection guard 都从 generated shape 读取 scheme / host / task path segment count / workspace query key，不再在 guard 中本地硬编码 `pt-workspace:`、`task` host 或 `workspace` query。official 与 browser prototype projection guard 仍会要求 URI task segment 与承载该 target 的 task id 一致、query workspace 与 `workspaceId` 一致，official Host response guard 也会拒绝 malformed workspace response shape；`atelier:workspace-open-controlled-gate` 进一步用 Desktop gateway Rust unit tests 证明 Host handler 只接受 canonical URI shape、拒绝 file/http/task mismatch/extra segment/missing workspace/fragment/credentials/port 等 non-contract URI，并且 controlled path 不启动 native IDE。official 与 browser prototype UI 都显式披露这是 Host intent only，不暴露 `file://`、本机绝对路径、shell 或 execute；`atelier:official-frontend-gate`、`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 与 `atelier:projection-contract-gate` 已固定 malformed prefixed URI / taskId mismatch / workspaceId mismatch reject matrix、generated URI shape guard、boundary 文案与 forbidden capability。真实本机 IDE launch、workspace resolver、sandbox runtime 与 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E 未验证。
- 产物预览面板：browser prototype 现在与 official projection boundary 对齐为 metadata-only preview，不再从 projection `Artifact` 类型或 mock seed 暴露/渲染 `markdown/content/diff/url/src` raw body/ref 字段，也不再在 preview panel 直接 `<iframe>` / `<img>` 渲染 raw URL/source。面板顶部保留 Host safe text capability 与 Host sandbox preview capability 区块，用于表达 official applet 当前只能通过 `atelier.artifact.body.fetch` 拉取 safe text body，并可通过 `atelier.artifact.preview.open` 提交 `previewTarget.mode=sandbox_manifest` / `atelier-sandbox://...` / `artifact://<task_id>/<artifact_id>/body` 的 Host intent；metadata-only preview 只展示 artifact kind/meta、projected paths、size 与 mock logs disclosure。official applet 只展示 `previewHint/bodyRef/bodyHash/bodySize/bodyKind/previewTarget/paths/size` 等 metadata，且 official / browser prototype projection guard 都会拒绝 top-level `Artifact.bodyRef=file://...` / `https://...` / `artifact://task-1/body` 这类非 canonical body ref，也会拒绝空 `previewTarget` 或缺 `mode/sandboxRef/bodyRef` 的不完整 preview descriptor；runtime logs 未接真实 `Run runtime stream` 的提示、iframe/image/html rich renderer 未接 Host sandbox renderer runtime 的提示，以及 preview.open Host renderer surface 状态（`desktop_host` / `host_sandbox_manifest` / `rendered`）；official client 与 browser applet-sdk bridge response guard 都会要求 Host response 对应当前 request：`taskId` / `artifactId` / `bodyRef` 必须一致，body fetch 在传入 `expectedHash` 时还要求 `bodyHash` 一致，preview open 还要求 `sandboxRef` 一致；browser applet-sdk bridge response guard 也会要求 service/Host response 为 `accepted=true`、`prepared=true`、`rendererSessionId=atelier-preview:*`、`rendererOwner=desktop_host`、`rendererMode=host_sandbox_manifest`、`rendererStatus` 属于 generated `allowedRendererStatus`（当前含 `prepared_not_opened` / `rendered`），拒绝 canonical refs 但 renderer 语义越界或 request/response 不一致的响应；Desktop Gateway 同时发出 Host UI side-effect `openAtelierArtifactPreview` 并要求 Host 返回 typed result，Lynx Host 消费后由 Desktop Host adapter 校验/记录 opaque renderer surface、拒绝 raw render fields，再把 Host-owned renderer response 返回 applet。这仍不代表 official/browser 已支持 iframe/image/html 渲染或 Console Logs 真实 runtime stream，official/prototype projection contract/guard 会拒绝 raw `markdown/content/body/html/diff/patch/url/src/iframe` 泄漏，也会拒绝非 `sandbox_manifest`、非 `atelier-sandbox://`、非 canonical `artifact://<task_id>/<artifact_id>/body` 的 preview target。

browser prototype artifact preview raw field hardening 当前有 controlled/static evidence：`packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier/src/types.ts` 的 `Artifact` 不再暴露 `markdown/content/diff/url/src`，`src/mock.ts` 不再 seed raw artifact body/ref，`src/preview.tsx` 只渲染 `ArtifactMetadataPreview`、Host safe text fetch 与 Host sandbox preview intent，不再包含 `<iframe>`、`<img>`、`artifact.markdown`、`artifact.url` 或 `artifact.src`。formal `model/domain/atelier/v1/projection.proto` 的 `AtelierArtifactProjection` 也 reserve 完整 contract forbidden raw field names：`markdown/content/body/html/diff/patch/url/src/iframe`；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 用 artifact projection block-level checks 固定 Go/TS proto generated output 不暴露这些字段/getter/tag。该证据只证明 browser prototype 与 formal proto 不再保留 raw projection body/ref render path，不证明 Host sandbox visual renderer、真实 artifact body fetch/render E2E、Console Logs 真实 runtime stream 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official safe markdown preview 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official `ArtifactPreviewPanel` 的 fetched body 展示已抽为 `SafeTextPreview`，只消费 Host `atelier.artifact.body.fetch` 返回的 safe `body.text`；`bodyKind=markdown` 时仅将 heading / list / code 做 text-only 行级展示，link、image、HTML、iframe、raw URL 与执行能力继续禁用，非 markdown body 仍走 safe text boundary 文案。official safe text preview 只本地展开前 80 行，并在超过该上限时显示 `+N` hidden safe text line disclosure，避免把本地 preview limit 误读为 Host body fetch 完整展示。official controller 在调用 `atelier.artifact.body.fetch` / `atelier.artifact.preview.open` 前也会再次校验 canonical `artifact://<task>/<artifact>/body`、canonical `atelier-sandbox://<task>/<artifact>/preview` 与 `mode=sandbox_manifest`，避免绕过 projection ingress。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 `SafeTextPreview`、80-line local overflow disclosure、safe markdown/text boundary locale 与 controller preflight guard，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official page/controller anchors 以及 raw media render 禁止项。该证据只证明 official safe-text display、本地行数上限 disclosure 与 controller-side preflight guard，不证明 iframe/image/html rich renderer、Host sandbox visual runtime、Console Logs 真实 runtime stream、真实 artifact body fetch E2E 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

Official artifact body / preview async state 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official `useAtelierController` 在发起 `atelier.artifact.body.fetch` 与 `atelier.artifact.preview.open` 时分别记录 `artifactBodyRequestSeq` / `artifactPreviewOpenRequestSeq`，并在 success/error 回来写入 `artifactBody` 或 `artifactPreviewOpenResponse` 前二次校验当前 pending key 仍为发起时的 `${taskId}:${artifactId}`；若用户切换 task/artifact、projection `artifact.upsert` invalidation 清空 pending key，或同一 artifact 后续请求已经成为最新 owner，旧 response/error 只 `return current`，不会覆盖当前 artifact panel。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 固定 body fetch 与 preview open 的 request owner guard；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 official controller 与 gate anchors。该证据只证明 official frontend local async state hygiene，不改变 Host response truth、Station artifact blob truth、Host sandbox renderer，也不证明真实 artifact body fetch/open E2E 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

metadata；`memoryCandidate.feeds[]` / `rerunIntent.feeds[]` 也不再由 frontend/prototype 维护本地 `planner/risk/verifier` union，而是由 project surface `memoryCandidateFeeds` 生成 `AtelierMemoryCandidateFeed`；`station_memory_review` 与 `station_rerun_review` confirmation mode 也不再由 controller/prototype 本地硬编码比较，而是由 confirmation method payload 的 `allowedConfirmationMode` 生成 `ATELIER_MEMORY_CONFIRMATION_MODE` / `ATELIER_RERUN_CONFIRMATION_MODE`。positive candidate 反哺 `planner/verifier`，negative candidate 反哺 `planner/risk/verifier`，并在 response 与 UI 中显示需要 memory confirmation，可继续提交 `atelier.memory.confirmCandidate` / `memory.confirmCandidate` confirmation intent；Station 只从 durable feedback event 读取 candidate payload，再通过 `MemoryService` 写长期 memory，不允许 applet 携带任意 memory content 或调用 `memory.write`；`atelier:feedback-memory-consumption-controlled-gate` 进一步用 Station service tests 证明 confirmed Atelier feedback memory 可被 `MemoryService.Search` 以 planner/risk/verifier query 检索，并进入 prompt relevant memory snapshot，但不证明 live Planner/Risk/Verifier model run 消费。regenerate 会显示需要 rerun review，并可继续提交 `atelier.feedback.confirmRerun` / `feedback.confirmRerun` confirmation intent；Station 只从 durable feedback event 读取 rerun intent，再由 orchestration 创建新的 collaboration task/new Run，不允许 applet 调用 `atelier.rerun`、`execute` 或 `run`。official controller 在 memory/rerun confirmation 失败时会保留 pending feedback id、task id 与 block id；browser prototype 也把 pending confirmation 绑定到产生 review 的 task id 与 block id，并在失败时显式保留 pending feedback id、task id 与 block id；official 与 browser prototype 都把 feedback status 绑定到源 block，只在源 block 展示 feedback result / Station review affordance 供重试，不把失败误解释为 review 已完成，也不因切换任务或渲染其它 agent block 把 confirmation intent/affordance/status 关联到错误对象；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 现在截取 official `FeedbackBar` 与 `submitFeedback` / `confirmMemoryCandidate` / `confirmRerun` flow source，固定 UI 只调用 callbacks 和 service-bound client `submitAtelierFeedback`、`confirmAtelierMemoryCandidate`、`confirmAtelierFeedbackRerun`，并拒绝 direct `memory.write`、`atelier.rerun`、`runtime.rerun`、`runtime.execute`、`provider.invoke` 出现在 official FeedbackBar / confirmation flow source；`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 现在截取 browser prototype `FeedbackBar` 与 `submitFeedback` / `confirmMemoryCandidate` / `confirmRerun` flow source，固定 UI 只调用 callback 和 `runtime.submitFeedback`、`runtime.confirmMemoryCandidate`、`runtime.confirmRerun`，并拒绝 direct `memory.write`、`atelier.rerun`、`runtime.rerun`、`execute`、`provider.invoke` 出现在 FeedbackBar / confirmation flow source；browser applet-sdk bridge 已把 `atelier.memory.confirmCandidate` / `atelier.feedback.confirmRerun` 归类为 non-snapshot Station confirmation response，并经 typed response guard 校验后返回，不再误执行 projection snapshot assertion；同一 bridge 也会校验 provider capabilities、feedback submit、workspace open、artifact body fetch 与 artifact preview open response shape，malformed Host response 会 fail closed；browser bridge runtime 也用显式 `NonSnapshotAtelierRuntimeMethod` / `SnapshotAtelierRuntimeMethod` 类型边界把这些 non-snapshot method 排除出 snapshot call，并在成功后恢复原状态。真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet memory/rerun confirmation E2E、live Planner/Risk/Verifier model 消费 E2E 和完整 E2E 未验证。
- Official / prototype non-artifact capability response guard 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official client 与 browser applet-sdk bridge 都要求 `atelier.feedback.submit`、`atelier.memory.confirmCandidate`、`atelier.feedback.confirmRerun`、`atelier.workspace.open` service/Host response 为 `accepted=true`；projection contract gate exact 校验 `methodPayloads` keys 只允许 workspace service load 与 projection event subscription 保持 payloadless，其余 method 必须声明 payload metadata，并 exact 校验 `atelier.provider.capabilities`、`atelier.feedback.submit`、`atelier.memory.confirmCandidate`、`atelier.feedback.confirmRerun`、`atelier.workspace.open` 的 responseFields、requiredFields、optionalFields、allowedSignals/allowedUriSchemes/uriShape 与 forbiddenActions，防止 contract 增加未被 official/prototype guard 处理的成功字段或 applet 不该拥有的输入/动作；`atelier.feedback.submit` 的 contract `responseFields` 显式包含 `memoryCandidate.status/reason/feeds` 与 `rerunIntent.status/reason/feeds`，与 official/prototype policy hint guard 对齐；`feedbackId` 与 policy hint `status/reason/confirmationMode` 为 non-empty string，`requiresConfirmation` 为 boolean，`memoryCandidate.feeds[]` / `rerunIntent.feeds[]` 只允许 project surface generated `ATELIER_MEMORY_CANDIDATE_FEEDS`，official `isAtelierMemoryCandidateFeed` 与 browser `isFeedbackFeed` 都通过 generated tuple membership 校验，不维护本地 `planner/risk/verifier` allowlist；workspace open response 的 URI scheme/host/task path segment count/workspace query key 由 generated `ATELIER_WORKSPACE_OPEN_URI_SHAPE` 校验；`atelier.memory.confirmCandidate` 的 `feedbackId/memoryId/status/source`、`atelier.feedback.confirmRerun` 的 `feedbackId/taskId/rerunTaskId/status/source`、`atelier.workspace.open` 的 `mode/reason` 也必须为 non-empty string。memory confirmation response 的 `feedbackId` 必须匹配 request，rerun confirmation response 的 `feedbackId/taskId` 必须匹配 request，workspace open response 的 `workspaceUri` 必须匹配 request；shared `nonArtifactCapabilities` malformed fixtures 已覆盖 wrong feedback id、wrong task id、wrong workspace uri、empty confirmation mode、non-boolean confirmation requirement 与 rerun forbidden feed。该证据只证明 applet/client response guard parity，不证明真实 memory write、rerun task creation、workspace IDE launch、本机 resolver 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
- P4-09 feedback submit Station ingress evidence：`atelier:feedback-submit-ingress-controlled-gate` 已把 `methodPayloads['atelier.feedback.submit'].controlledEvidence` 补强为 `stationIngressForbiddenFieldGuardProven=true` 与 `stationPolicyEventPayloadProven=true`；official Station façade 对 `/v1/feedback/submit` 拒绝 `run/execute/provider/attachments/memory/memoryContent/memory_content/rerun/rerunTaskId/inputSnapshot/input_snapshot`，Station `SubmitFeedback` 持久化的 typed feedback event 只保存 `signal/comment` 与 Station-owned `memory_candidate_*` / `rerun_intent_*` policy hint metadata。该证据不证明真实 memory write E2E、真实 rerun task creation E2E、Planner/Risk/Verifier feedback consumption 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

- 长任务 replay 已补基础聚合：Station snapshot 会合并早期事件、最新尾部事件，以及 artifact / gate / decision replay anchors，避免关键 projection 被有限首段 event page 截断；同时返回每个 task 的 replay metadata。official 与 browser prototype projection guard 都会拒绝空 task replay key、空 `source`、负数 cursor、空 `checkpointId` 与负数 `checkpointEventSeq`，避免 malformed replay cursor 进入 subscribe/recovery surface。checkpoint 写入侧已生成 `TaskCheckpoint.StateJSON.atelierProjection`，读取侧已支持消费 materialized projection 并只叠加 checkpoint 后事件，wrong-version checkpoint projection 会 fail closed；Desktop Host 已持久化 `atelier.projection.event` cursor。Official subscription cleanup 当前有 controlled/local evidence：`subscribeAtelierProjectionEvents` 在 Station stream subscribe 失败和 release cleanup 两条路径都会先移除 local `sdk.events.on` handler，再通过 `safeUnsubscribeAtelierProjectionEventTopic()` 调 `sdk.events.unsubscribe(ATELIER_PROJECTION_EVENT_TOPIC).catch(...)`，避免 Host unsubscribe rejection 形成 unhandled rejection；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 已执行成功订阅、release cleanup、Station stream subscribe reject 后 cleanup、reject 后的 Host event 不会进入 official handler、以及 unsubscribe rejection warning cases。Browser prototype bridge runtime 也有 controlled/local evidence：`testAppletBridgeCanonicalSubscriptionRejectionCleansLocalEventHandler` 证明 canonical `atelier.events.subscribe` reject 会清理本地 event handler，`evt-after-canonical-subscription-rejection` 不会进入 runtime listener。当前证据仍是 controlled Station tests / local gates，不等于真实跨重启 cursor-based replay、任意网络故障恢复或真实 product window E2E。
- P4-158 official generated `agentIds[0]` strict fallback 当前有 controlled/local evidence：official projection stream resolver 现在通过 `agentIdAtSourceIndex(source, 0)` 只读取原始第一项并 trim，不再把 `agentIds` 过滤成 first non-empty list 后取值；因此空白 explicit `agentId` 仍可 fallback 到非空 `agentIds[0]`，但 `agentIds: ['   ', 'agent-ignored']` 会 fail-closed，不会把 `agent-ignored` 发送给 `atelier.events.subscribe`。`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 同时覆盖 trimmed first-entry fallback 与 empty first-entry no Station subscribe，`atelier-projection-contract-gate` 固定 strict indexed resolver 与 gate cases。该证据只证明 controlled/local official applet subscribe config policy，不证明真实 Desktop Host launch config、真实 Station SSE 网络恢复、跨重启 cursor recovery、真实 product-window UI、post-ready UI actions、real IDE launch、production provider/runtime quality 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

- 移动端单栏收敛已有 controlled/local evidence：official `AtelierAppletPage.tsx` 的 workspace body 从固定三栏收敛为 `flexDirection: 'column'`，左侧任务/中央信息流/右侧 projection 面板均以 `width: '100%'` + `minWidth: 0` 堆叠，保留原有 section title 作为单栏分段边界；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定这些 layout anchors，并拒绝重新引入中央信息流的 desktop-only `minWidth: px(360)`。该证据只证明 official frontend presentation 的单栏 readiness，不证明真实 Mobile Host、真实设备渲染、Desktop product window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
